# حزمة أكواد موقع WW



> ملف مولّد تلقائيًا من ملفات المشروع. عدّل الملفات الأصلية، وسيُحدّث هذا الملف عند حفظ التغييرات إذا كانت مهمة المراقبة تعمل.



آخر تحديث: 2026-10-05T21:25:07.695Z

```html
<!doctype html><html lang="ar" dir="rtl"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><meta name="description" content="تعرف على طريقة عمل WW في تنسيق النطاقات والمواقع وبريد الأعمال للأعمال."><title>عن WW | خدمات رقمية</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="styles.css"><link rel="stylesheet" href="service-pages.css"><link rel="alternate" hreflang="en" href="en/about.html"></head><body><header class="site-header"><div class="container nav-wrap no-toggle"><a class="brand" href="index.html"><span class="brand-mark"><svg viewBox="0 0 64 52"><path d="M4 13 10 39 17 22 24 39 31 13M33 13 39 39 46 22 53 39 60 13"/></svg></span><span class="brand-name"><strong>WW</strong><small>DIGITAL SERVICES</small></span></a><nav class="main-nav"><a href="index.html">الرئيسية</a><a href="portfolio.html">النماذج</a><a href="terms.html">السياسات</a><a href="en/about.html" lang="en">English</a></nav></div></header><main><section class="inner-hero"><div class="container"><div class="eyebrow">عن WW</div><h1>أساس رقمي متناسق، بخطوات مفهومة.</h1><p>WW علامة لخدمات رقمية تساعد أصحاب الأعمال على تنسيق النطاق والموقع وبريد الأعمال ضمن تجربة واضحة من الطلب إلى الإعداد والتسليم.</p></div></section><section class="page-content"><div class="container"><div class="detail-grid"><article class="detail-card"><h2>ما الذي نقدمه؟</h2><p>خدمات النطاقات، إنشاء المواقع، وإعداد بريد الأعمال عبر Google Workspace أو Microsoft 365 وفق الباقات المتاحة.</p></article><article class="detail-card"><h2>كيف نعمل؟</h2><p>نبدأ بفهم احتياجك، ثم نوضح نطاق العمل والسعر والمتطلبات كتابيًا قبل التنفيذ، ونتابع معك مراحل التجهيز والتسليم.</p></article><article class="detail-card"><h2>ما الذي نلتزم به؟</h2><p>شرح واضح للرسوم والاشتراكات المتكررة، وحماية بيانات الطلب، وعدم الادعاء بنتائج أو اعتمادات غير موثقة.</p></article></div></div></section></main></body></html>
```

```html
<!doctype html><html lang="ar" dir="rtl"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><meta name="description" content="دليل مسؤوليات ملكية النطاق والاستضافة والبريد وتسليم الصلاحيات."><title>الملكية والصلاحيات | WW</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="styles.css"><link rel="stylesheet" href="service-pages.css"></head><body><header class="site-header"><div class="container nav-wrap no-toggle"><a class="brand" href="index.html"><span class="brand-mark"><svg viewBox="0 0 64 52"><path d="M4 13 10 39 17 22 24 39 31 13M33 13 39 39 46 22 53 39 60 13"/></svg></span><span class="brand-name"><strong>WW</strong><small>DIGITAL SERVICES</small></span></a><nav class="main-nav"><a href="index.html">الرئيسية</a><a href="knowledge.html">مركز المعرفة</a><a href="en/account-ownership.html" lang="en">English</a></nav></div></header><main><section class="inner-hero"><div class="container"><div class="eyebrow">الملكية والصلاحيات</div><h1>احتفظ بالتحكم في أصولك الرقمية.</h1><p>قبل بدء العمل، اتفق كتابةً على مالك الحسابات وطريقة تسليم الصلاحيات والاسترداد.</p></div></section><section class="page-content"><div class="container"><div class="detail-grid"><article class="detail-card"><h2>النطاق</h2><p>استخدم بيانات مالك صحيحة وبريدًا تملكه، واحتفظ ببيانات دخول جهة التسجيل ووسيلة استرداد الحساب.</p></article><article class="detail-card"><h2>الاستضافة والموقع</h2><p>حدد من يدير التجديد والنسخ الاحتياطي والتحديثات، ومن يملك ملفات الموقع ولوحة الإدارة وفق عرض العمل.</p></article><article class="detail-card"><h2>البريد</h2><p>وثّق مسؤول النظام وطرق الاسترداد وعدد التراخيص، ولا تشارك كلمات المرور في نموذج التواصل.</p></article></div><div class="detail-card"><h2>عند التسليم</h2><p>راجع قائمة الخدمات المسلّمة، والصلاحيات، ووسائل الاسترداد، والتجديدات القادمة. توضح وثيقة العرض المسؤوليات الخاصة بمشروعك.</p><a class="text-link" href="aftercare.html">افتح دليل ما بعد التسليم ←</a></div></div></section></main></body></html>
```

```html
<body>

      <a class="admin-brand" href="/"><span class="admin-mark" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 64 52"><path d="M4 13 10 39 17 22 24 39 31 13M33 13 39 39 46 22 53 39 60 13"/></svg></span><span><b>WW</b><small>لوحة الطلبات</small></span></a>
      <div><a class="quiet-button" href="/en/admin.html" lang="en">English</a> <button class="quiet-button" id="logout-button" type="button" hidden>تسجيل الخروج</button></div>
    </header>
    <section class="login-panel" id="login-panel">
      <span class="panel-kicker">مساحة خاصة</span>
      <h1>مرحبًا بك</h1>
      <p>سجّل الدخول للاطلاع على طلبات العملاء.</p>
      <form id="login-form">
        <label for="admin-password">كلمة مرور الإدارة</label>
        <input id="admin-password" name="password" type="password" autocomplete="current-password" required>
        <button class="primary-button" type="submit">دخول إلى الطلبات <span>←</span></button>
      </form>
      <p class="feedback" id="login-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p>

      <div class="orders-heading"><div><span class="panel-kicker">مساحة خاصة</span><h1>طلبات العملاء</h1><p>كل الطلبات المرسلة من نموذج الموقع محفوظة هنا.</p></div><button class="quiet-button" id="refresh-button" type="button">تحديث القائمة ↻</button></div>
      <div class="orders-summary"><span>إجمالي الطلبات</span><strong id="orders-count">0</strong></div>
      <p class="feedback" id="orders-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p>
      <div class="orders-list" id="orders-list"></div>
      <section class="content-editor"><div class="orders-heading"><div><span class="panel-kicker">إدارة المحتوى</span><h2>تحديث الأسعار والأسئلة والإعلان</h2><p>التعديلات المنشورة تظهر للعملاء في صفحات الأسعار والأسئلة والتحديثات.</p></div></div><form id="content-form"><h3>إعلان الموقع</h3><label><input name="announcementActive" type="checkbox"> نشر الإعلان</label><div class="content-columns"><label>عنوان الإعلان بالعربية<input name="announcementArTitle" maxlength="120"></label><label>نص الإعلان بالعربية<textarea name="announcementArBody" maxlength="1000"></textarea></label><label>Announcement title in English<input name="announcementEnTitle" maxlength="120"></label><label>Announcement text in English<textarea name="announcementEnBody" maxlength="1000"></textarea></label></div><h3>سؤال شائع مخصص</h3><div class="content-columns"><label>السؤال بالعربية<input name="faqArQuestion" maxlength="220"></label><label>الإجابة بالعربية<textarea name="faqArAnswer" maxlength="1000"></textarea></label><label>Question in English<input name="faqEnQuestion" maxlength="220"></label><label>Answer in English<textarea name="faqEnAnswer" maxlength="1000"></textarea></label></div><h3>الأسعار بالريال</h3><p class="content-help">تحدّث هذه القيم صفحة المنتجات والحاسبة. راجع الأسعار قبل النشر.</p><div class="price-editor" id="price-editor"></div><button class="primary-button" type="submit">حفظ ونشر المحتوى</button><p class="feedback" id="content-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p></form></section>
    </section>
  </main>
</body>
</html>
```

## admin.js

```js
const loginPanel = document.querySelector('#login-panel');
const ordersPanel = document.querySelector('#orders-panel');
const loginForm = document.querySelector('#login-form');
const loginFeedback = document.querySelector('#login-feedback');
const ordersFeedback = document.querySelector('#orders-feedback');
const ordersList = document.querySelector('#orders-list');
const ordersCount = document.querySelector('#orders-count');
const logoutButton = document.querySelector('#logout-button');
const englishAdmin = document.documentElement.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith('en');
const adminText = (arabic, english) => englishAdmin ? english : arabic;
const priceDefaults = { domainSaNew:100,domainSaRenew:100,domainSaRecovery:150,domainSaDirect:199,domainSaTransfer:100,domainGlobalNew:75,domainGlobalRenew:75,domainGlobalTransfer:80,googleStarter:390,googleStandard:800,googlePlus:1200,microsoftBasic:399,microsoftStandard:790,microsoftPremium:1200,microsoftCopilot:1600,websitePremium:1500 };
const priceLabels = { domainSaNew:['تسجيل نطاق سعودي','Saudi domain registration'],domainSaRenew:['تجديد نطاق سعودي','Saudi domain renewal'],domainSaRecovery:['استعادة نطاق سعودي','Saudi domain recovery'],domainSaDirect:['التجديد المباشر السعودي','Saudi direct renewal'],domainSaTransfer:['نقل نطاق سعودي','Saudi domain transfer'],domainGlobalNew:['تسجيل نطاق عالمي','Global domain registration'],domainGlobalRenew:['تجديد نطاق عالمي','Global domain renewal'],domainGlobalTransfer:['نقل نطاق عالمي','Global domain transfer'],googleStarter:['Google Workspace Starter','Google Workspace Starter'],googleStandard:['Google Workspace Standard','Google Workspace Standard'],googlePlus:['Google Workspace Plus','Google Workspace Plus'],microsoftBasic:['Microsoft 365 Basic','Microsoft 365 Basic'],microsoftStandard:['Microsoft 365 Standard','Microsoft 365 Standard'],microsoftPremium:['Microsoft 365 Premium','Microsoft 365 Premium'],microsoftCopilot:['Microsoft 365 Copilot','Microsoft 365 Copilot'],websitePremium:['باقة إنشاء المواقع','Website creation package'] };

async function api(url, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(url, { credentials: 'same-origin', ...options });
  const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!response.ok) {
    if (englishAdmin && response.status === 401) throw new Error('Please sign in to view requests.');
    const message = data.error || 'تعذر إكمال الطلب.';
    throw new Error(englishAdmin && /[\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(message) ? 'The request could not be completed. Please try again.' : message);
  }
  return data;
}

function addDetail(container, label, value, extraClass = '') {
  const row = document.createElement('div');
  row.className = `order-detail ${extraClass}`.trim();
  const key = document.createElement('b');
  key.textContent = `${label}:`;
  const text = document.createElement('span');
  text.textContent = value || '—';
  row.append(key, text);
  container.append(row);
}

function ensureContentEditor() {
  let form = document.querySelector('#content-form');
  if (form) return form;
  const section = document.createElement('section'); section.className = 'content-editor';
  const title = englishAdmin ? 'Content management' : 'إدارة المحتوى';
  const heading = document.createElement('h2'); heading.textContent = englishAdmin ? 'Edit prices, FAQ and announcement' : 'تحديث الأسعار والأسئلة والإعلان';
  const description = document.createElement('p'); description.textContent = englishAdmin ? 'Changes are published on the relevant site pages.' : 'تظهر التعديلات في صفحات الموقع المرتبطة.';
  form = document.createElement('form'); form.id = 'content-form';
  form.innerHTML = englishAdmin
    ? '<h3>Announcement</h3><label><input name="announcementActive" type="checkbox"> Publish announcement</label><div class="content-columns"><label>Arabic title<input name="announcementArTitle" maxlength="120"></label><label>Arabic text<textarea name="announcementArBody" maxlength="1000"></textarea></label><label>English title<input name="announcementEnTitle" maxlength="120"></label><label>English text<textarea name="announcementEnBody" maxlength="1000"></textarea></label></div><h3>Custom FAQ</h3><div class="content-columns"><label>Arabic question<input name="faqArQuestion" maxlength="220"></label><label>Arabic answer<textarea name="faqArAnswer" maxlength="1000"></textarea></label><label>English question<input name="faqEnQuestion" maxlength="220"></label><label>English answer<textarea name="faqEnAnswer" maxlength="1000"></textarea></label></div><h3>Prices in SAR</h3><div class="content-help">Prices below update product pages and the calculator.</div><div class="price-editor" id="price-editor"></div><button class="primary-button" type="submit">Save and publish</button><p class="feedback" id="content-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p>'
    : '<h3>الإعلان</h3><label><input name="announcementActive" type="checkbox"> نشر الإعلان</label><div class="content-columns"><label>عنوان عربي<input name="announcementArTitle" maxlength="120"></label><label>نص عربي<textarea name="announcementArBody" maxlength="1000"></textarea></label><label>عنوان إنجليزي<input name="announcementEnTitle" maxlength="120"></label><label>نص إنجليزي<textarea name="announcementEnBody" maxlength="1000"></textarea></label></div><h3>سؤال شائع مخصص</h3><div class="content-columns"><label>السؤال بالعربية<input name="faqArQuestion" maxlength="220"></label><label>الإجابة بالعربية<textarea name="faqArAnswer" maxlength="1000"></textarea></label><label>السؤال بالإنجليزية<input name="faqEnQuestion" maxlength="220"></label><label>الإجابة بالإنجليزية<textarea name="faqEnAnswer" maxlength="1000"></textarea></label></div><h3>الأسعار بالريال</h3><div class="content-help">تحدّث هذه الأسعار صفحة المنتجات والحاسبة.</div><div class="price-editor" id="price-editor"></div><button class="primary-button" type="submit">حفظ ونشر</button><p class="feedback" id="content-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p>';
  section.append(heading, description, form); ordersPanel.append(section); return form;
}

async function loadSiteContent() {
  const form = ensureContentEditor(); const editor = form.querySelector('#price-editor');
  editor.replaceChildren();
  for (const [key, label] of Object.entries(priceLabels)) { const wrapper = document.createElement('label'); wrapper.textContent = label[englishAdmin ? 1 : 0]; const input = document.createElement('input'); input.type = 'number'; input.min = '0'; input.max = '100000'; input.step = '1'; input.name = `price:${key}`; input.required = true; wrapper.append(input); editor.append(wrapper); }
  form.addEventListener('submit', saveSiteContent, { once: true });
  try {
    const data = await api('/api/site-content'); const content = data.content || {};
    for (const name of ['announcementArTitle','announcementArBody','announcementEnTitle','announcementEnBody','faqArQuestion','faqArAnswer','faqEnQuestion','faqEnAnswer']) form.elements[name].value = content[name] || '';
    form.elements.announcementActive.checked = content.announcementActive === 'true' || content.announcementActive === true;
    const prices = { ...priceDefaults, ...(content.prices || {}) };
    for (const key of Object.keys(priceDefaults)) form.elements[`price:${key}`].value = prices[key];
  } catch (error) { document.querySelector('#content-feedback').textContent = error.message; }
}

async function saveSiteContent(event) {
  event.preventDefault(); const form = event.currentTarget; const feedback = form.querySelector('#content-feedback');
  const content = Object.fromEntries(['announcementArTitle','announcementArBody','announcementEnTitle','announcementEnBody','faqArQuestion','faqArAnswer','faqEnQuestion','faqEnAnswer'].map((key) => [key, form.elements[key].value]));
  content.announcementActive = form.elements.announcementActive.checked;
  content.prices = Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(priceDefaults).map((key) => [key, Number(form.elements[`price:${key}`].value)]));
  try { await api('/api/site-content', { method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ content }) }); feedback.textContent = adminText('تم نشر المحتوى والأسعار.', 'Content and prices published.'); form.addEventListener('submit', saveSiteContent, { once: true }); }
  catch (error) { feedback.textContent = error.message; form.addEventListener('submit', saveSiteContent, { once: true }); }
}

function renderOrders(orders) {
  ordersCount.textContent = String(orders.length);
  ordersList.replaceChildren();
  if (!orders.length) {
    const empty = document.createElement('div');
    empty.className = 'empty-state';
    empty.textContent = adminText('لا توجد طلبات حتى الآن. ستظهر طلبات العملاء الجديدة هنا.', 'There are no requests yet. New customer requests will appear here.');
    ordersList.append(empty);
    return;
  }

  orders.forEach((order) => {
    const card = document.createElement('article');
    card.className = 'order-card';
    const top = document.createElement('div');
    top.className = 'order-top';
    const heading = document.createElement('div');
    const name = document.createElement('div');
    name.className = 'order-name';
    name.textContent = order.name;
    const service = document.createElement('span');
    service.className = 'order-service';
    const serviceNames = { 'إنشاء نطاق': 'Domain registration', 'إنشاء موقع': 'Website creation', 'بريد أعمال': 'Business email', 'جميع الخدمات': 'All services', 'تذكير بتجديد نطاق': 'Domain renewal reminder request', 'طلب موعد استشارة': 'Consultation request', 'طلب اتصال': 'Phone call request', 'تقييم خدمة': 'Customer feedback', 'طلب عرض سعر': 'Custom quote request', 'صيانة موقع': 'Website maintenance' };
    service.textContent = englishAdmin ? (serviceNames[order.service] || order.service) : order.service;
    const date = document.createElement('div');
    date.className = 'order-date';
    date.textContent = new Date(order.createdAt).toLocaleString(englishAdmin ? 'en-GB' : 'ar-SA', { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' });
    heading.append(name, service);
    const status = document.createElement('select');
    status.className = 'order-status-select';
    status.setAttribute('aria-label', adminText(`حالة طلب ${order.name}`, `Status for ${order.name}`));
    const statusOptions = [
      ['new', adminText('جديد', 'New')], ['reviewing', adminText('قيد المراجعة', 'Under review')], ['awaiting_customer', adminText('بانتظار العميل', 'Waiting for customer')],
      ['in_progress', adminText('قيد التنفيذ', 'In progress')], ['completed', adminText('مكتمل', 'Completed')], ['cancelled', adminText('ملغي', 'Cancelled')]
    ];
    statusOptions.forEach(([value, label]) => {
      const option = document.createElement('option');
      option.value = value; option.textContent = label; option.selected = (order.status || 'new') === value;
      status.append(option);
    });
    status.addEventListener('change', async () => {
      status.disabled = true;
      try {
        await api(`/api/orders/${encodeURIComponent(order.requestNumber)}/status`, { method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ status: status.value }) });
        ordersFeedback.textContent = adminText('تم تحديث حالة الطلب.', 'Request status updated.');
      } catch (error) { ordersFeedback.textContent = error.message; ordersFeedback.className = 'feedback error'; }
      finally { status.disabled = false; }
    });
    const remove = document.createElement('button');
    remove.type = 'button';
    remove.className = 'delete-button';
    remove.textContent = adminText('حذف الطلب', 'Delete request');
    remove.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      if (!window.confirm(adminText(`حذف طلب ${order.name}؟`, `Delete the request from ${order.name}?`))) return;
      try {
        await api(`/api/orders/${encodeURIComponent(order.requestNumber)}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
        await loadOrders();
      } catch (error) { ordersFeedback.textContent = error.message; ordersFeedback.className = 'feedback error'; }
    });
    const notify = document.createElement('button'); notify.type = 'button'; notify.className = 'quiet-button'; notify.textContent = adminText('نسخ رسالة الحالة', 'Copy status update');
    notify.addEventListener('click', async () => { const labels={new:adminText('تم استلام طلبك','We received your request'),reviewing:adminText('طلبك قيد المراجعة','Your request is under review'),awaiting_customer:adminText('نحتاج معلومات إضافية لمتابعة طلبك','We need more information to proceed'),in_progress:adminText('بدأنا العمل على طلبك','Work on your request has started'),completed:adminText('اكتمل طلبك','Your request is complete'),cancelled:adminText('تم إغلاق طلبك','Your request has been closed')};const link=`${location.origin}${englishAdmin?'/en/track-order.html':'/track-order.html'}?id=${encodeURIComponent(order.requestNumber)}`;const message=`${order.name}, ${labels[status.value]||labels[order.status]}. ${adminText('يمكنك متابعة الحالة من الرابط:','Track your request here:')} ${link}`;try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(message);ordersFeedback.textContent=adminText('نُسخت رسالة الحالة. أرسلها للعميل بعد مراجعتها.','Status message copied. Review and send it to the customer.');}catch{ordersFeedback.textContent=message;}});
    const meta = document.createElement('div');
    meta.append(date, status, notify, remove);
    meta.style.cssText = 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px';
    top.append(heading, meta);
    const details = document.createElement('div');
    details.className = 'order-details';
    addDetail(details, adminText('البريد', 'Email'), order.email);
    addDetail(details, adminText('الجوال', 'Mobile'), order.phone);
    addDetail(details, adminText('الموافقة على الشروط', 'Terms acceptance'), order.termsAccepted ? (englishAdmin ? `Accepted (${order.termsVersion || 'previous version'})` : `موافق عليها (${order.termsVersion || 'إصدار سابق'})`) : adminText('غير مسجلة', 'Not recorded'));
    if (order.message) addDetail(details, adminText('التفاصيل', 'Details'), order.message, 'order-message');
    for (const file of order.files || []) { const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='order-detail';const label=document.createElement('b');label.textContent=adminText('ملف مرفق:','File:');const link=document.createElement('a');link.href=`/api/customer-files/${encodeURIComponent(file.id)}`;link.textContent=`${file.name} (${Math.ceil(file.size/1024)} KB)`;link.setAttribute('download','');row.append(label,link);details.append(row); }
    card.append(top, details);
    const invite = document.createElement('button'); invite.type='button'; invite.className='order-notify'; invite.textContent=adminText('إنشاء رابط رفع ملفات','Create secure upload link');
    invite.addEventListener('click', async()=>{invite.disabled=true;try{const result=await api('/api/upload-invites',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({orderId:order.id})});const link=`${location.origin}${englishAdmin?'/en':'/'}file-upload.html?token=${encodeURIComponent(result.token)}`;await navigator.clipboard.writeText(link);ordersFeedback.textContent=adminText('تم إنشاء رابط رفع صالح لمدة 7 أيام ونسخه. أرسله للعميل من قناة التواصل المتفق عليها.','A seven-day upload link was created and copied. Send it to the customer through your agreed contact channel.');}catch(error){ordersFeedback.textContent=error.message;}finally{invite.disabled=false;}});
    card.append(invite);
    ordersList.append(card);
  });
}

async function loadOrders() {
  ordersFeedback.textContent = adminText('جارٍ تحميل الطلبات…', 'Loading requests…');
  ordersFeedback.className = 'feedback';
  try {
    const data = await api('/api/orders');
    renderOrders(data.orders);
    ordersFeedback.textContent = '';
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.message.includes('سجل الدخول') || error.message.includes('Please sign in')) showLogin();
    else { ordersFeedback.textContent = error.message; ordersFeedback.className = 'feedback error'; }
  }
}

function showLogin() {
  loginPanel.hidden = false;
  ordersPanel.hidden = true;
  logoutButton.hidden = true;
}

function showOrders() {
  loginPanel.hidden = true;
  ordersPanel.hidden = false;
  logoutButton.hidden = false;
  loadOrders();
  loadSiteContent();
}

loginForm.addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
  event.preventDefault();
  loginFeedback.textContent = adminText('جارٍ التحقق…', 'Checking…');
  loginFeedback.className = 'feedback';
  const password = new FormData(loginForm).get('password');
  try {
    await api('/api/session', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ password }) });
    loginForm.reset();
    showOrders();
  } catch (error) {
    loginFeedback.textContent = error.message;
    loginFeedback.className = 'feedback error';
  }
});

logoutButton.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  await api('/api/session', { method: 'DELETE' }).catch(() => {});
  showLogin();
});

document.querySelector('#refresh-button').addEventListener('click', loadOrders);

api('/api/orders').then((data) => { renderOrders(data.orders); showOrders(); })
  .catch(() => showLogin());
```

## cloudflare/worker.js

```js
﻿const SESSION_COOKIE = 'ww_admin';
const SESSION_TTL_SECONDS = 8 * 60 * 60;
const LOGIN_WINDOW_SECONDS = 15 * 60;
const LOGIN_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 8;
const MAX_BODY_BYTES = 12_000;
const encoder = new TextEncoder();

function json(value, status = 200, headers = {}) {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(value), {
    status,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
      'Cache-Control': 'no-store',
      'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',
      'Referrer-Policy': 'strict-origin-when-cross-origin',
      ...headers
    }
  });
}

async function readJson(request) {
  const size = Number(request.headers.get('content-length') || 0);
  if (size > MAX_BODY_BYTES) throw new Error('بيانات الطلب أكبر من الحجم المسموح.');
  const text = await request.text();
  if (encoder.encode(text).length > MAX_BODY_BYTES) throw new Error('بيانات الطلب أكبر من الحجم المسموح.');
  try { return JSON.parse(text || '{}'); }
  catch { throw new Error('بيانات الطلب غير صالحة.'); }
}

function cleanText(value, maxLength) {
  return typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim().slice(0, maxLength) : '';
}

function shortRequestNumber(id) {
  return `WW-${String(id).replaceAll('-', '').slice(0, 10).toUpperCase()}`;
}

function validateOrder(body) {
  const order = {
    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    name: cleanText(body.name, 160),
    email: cleanText(body.email, 254).toLowerCase(),
    phone: cleanText(body.phone, 30)
      .replace(/[٠-٩]/g, (digit) => String(digit.charCodeAt(0) - 0x0660))
      .replace(/[۰-۹]/g, (digit) => String(digit.charCodeAt(0) - 0x06f0)),
    service: cleanText(body.service, 80),
    message: cleanText(body.message, 3000),
    termsAccepted: body.termsAccepted === 'accepted',
    termsVersion: '2026-10-05-v3',
    createdAt: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)
  };
  const emailPattern = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
  const phonePattern = /^[+0-9 ()-]{8,30}$/;
  const services = new Set(['إنشاء نطاق', 'إنشاء موقع', 'بريد أعمال', 'جميع الخدمات', 'تذكير بتجديد نطاق', 'طلب موعد استشارة', 'طلب اتصال', 'تقييم خدمة', 'طلب عرض سعر', 'صيانة موقع']);
  if (order.name.length < 2 || !emailPattern.test(order.email) || !phonePattern.test(order.phone) || !services.has(order.service) || !order.termsAccepted) return null;
  return order;
}

function cookieToken(request) {
  const cookie = request.headers.get('Cookie') || '';
  const match = cookie.match(new RegExp(`(?:^|;\\s*)${SESSION_COOKIE}=([a-f0-9]{64})(?:;|$)`, 'i'));
  return match ? match[1] : '';
}

function sessionCookie(token, maxAge = SESSION_TTL_SECONDS) {
  return `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${token}; Path=/; Max-Age=${maxAge}; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Strict`;
}

async function hash(value) {
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', encoder.encode(value));
  return [...new Uint8Array(digest)].map((part) => part.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

function passwordMatches(supplied, expected) {
  const left = encoder.encode(String(supplied));
  const right = encoder.encode(String(expected));
  let difference = left.length ^ right.length;
  const length = Math.max(left.length, right.length);
  for (let index = 0; index < length; index += 1) difference |= (left[index] || 0) ^ (right[index] || 0);
  return difference === 0;
}

function isSameOrigin(request) {
  return request.headers.get('Origin') === new URL(request.url).origin;
}

async function authorized(request, env) {
  const token = cookieToken(request);
  if (!token) return false;
  const tokenHash = await hash(token);
  const session = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT expires_at FROM admin_sessions WHERE token_hash = ? AND expires_at > ?'
  ).bind(tokenHash, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)).first();
  return Boolean(session);
}

async function handleLogin(request, env) {
  if (!isSameOrigin(request)) return json({ error: 'مصدر الطلب غير صالح.' }, 403);
  if (!env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || env.ADMIN_PASSWORD.length < 16) {
    return json({ error: 'لم تضبط كلمة مرور لوحة الإدارة. أضف سر ADMIN_PASSWORD (16 حرفًا على الأقل) في إعدادات Worker.' }, 503);
  }

  let body;
  try { body = await readJson(request); }
  catch (error) { return json({ error: error.message }, 400); }
  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) body = {};

  const ip = request.headers.get('CF-Connecting-IP') || 'unknown';
  const visitorHash = await hash(ip);
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const attempt = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT failures, window_started FROM admin_login_attempts WHERE visitor_hash = ?'
  ).bind(visitorHash).first();
  if (attempt && attempt.window_started + LOGIN_WINDOW_SECONDS > now && attempt.failures >= LOGIN_MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
    return json({ error: 'محاولات كثيرة. حاول بعد 15 دقيقة.' }, 429);
  }

  if (!passwordMatches(body.password || '', env.ADMIN_PASSWORD)) {
    await env.DB.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO admin_login_attempts (visitor_hash, failures, window_started)
      VALUES (?, 1, ?)
      ON CONFLICT(visitor_hash) DO UPDATE SET
        failures = CASE
          WHEN admin_login_attempts.window_started + ? <= ? THEN 1
          ELSE admin_login_attempts.failures + 1
        END,
        window_started = CASE
          WHEN admin_login_attempts.window_started + ? <= ? THEN excluded.window_started
          ELSE admin_login_attempts.window_started
        END
    `).bind(visitorHash, now, LOGIN_WINDOW_SECONDS, now, LOGIN_WINDOW_SECONDS, now).run();
    return json({ error: 'كلمة المرور غير صحيحة.' }, 401);
  }

  const token = [...crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32))].map((part) => part.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
  const expiresAt = now + SESSION_TTL_SECONDS;
  await env.DB.batch([
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM admin_login_attempts WHERE visitor_hash = ?').bind(visitorHash),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM admin_sessions WHERE expires_at <= ?').bind(now),
    env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO admin_sessions (token_hash, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?)').bind(await hash(token), expiresAt)
  ]);
  return json({ ok: true }, 200, { 'Set-Cookie': sessionCookie(token) });
}

async function handleRequest(request, env) {
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  if (url.pathname === '/health' && request.method === 'GET') return json({ ok: true });

  if (url.pathname === '/api/status' && request.method === 'GET') {
    let database = 'operational';
    try { await env.DB.prepare('SELECT 1 AS ok').first(); } catch { database = 'unavailable'; }
    return json({ status: database === 'operational' ? 'operational' : 'degraded', checkedAt: new Date().toISOString(), services: { website: 'operational', requests: database, emailNotifications: 'not-configured', payments: 'not-configured', secureFilePortal: env.CUSTOMER_FILES ? 'operational' : 'not-configured' } });
  }

  if (url.pathname === '/api/site-content' && request.method === 'GET') {
    const result = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT content_key, content_value, updated_at FROM site_content').all();
    const content = Object.fromEntries((result.results || []).map((row) => [row.content_key, row.content_value]));
    const lastUpdate = result.results?.find((row) => row.content_key === 'announcementActive');
    if (lastUpdate) content.updatedAt = new Date(lastUpdate.updated_at * 1000).toISOString();
    if (content.prices) { try { content.prices = JSON.parse(content.prices); } catch { content.prices = {}; } }
    return json({ content });
  }

  if (url.pathname === '/api/site-content' && request.method === 'PUT') {
    if (!isSameOrigin(request)) return json({ error: 'مصدر الطلب غير صالح.' }, 403);
    if (!await authorized(request, env)) return json({ error: 'سجل الدخول أولًا.' }, 401);
    let body;
    try { body = await readJson(request); } catch (error) { return json({ error: error.message }, 400); }
    const content = body?.content;
    if (!content || typeof content !== 'object' || Array.isArray(content)) return json({ error: 'بيانات المحتوى غير صالحة.' }, 400);
    const clean = {
      announcementArTitle: cleanText(content.announcementArTitle, 120), announcementArBody: cleanText(content.announcementArBody, 1000),
      announcementEnTitle: cleanText(content.announcementEnTitle, 120), announcementEnBody: cleanText(content.announcementEnBody, 1000),
      faqArQuestion: cleanText(content.faqArQuestion, 220), faqArAnswer: cleanText(content.faqArAnswer, 1000),
      faqEnQuestion: cleanText(content.faqEnQuestion, 220), faqEnAnswer: cleanText(content.faqEnAnswer, 1000),
      announcementActive: content.announcementActive === true ? 'true' : 'false'
    };
    const priceKeys = ['domainSaNew','domainSaRenew','domainSaRecovery','domainSaDirect','domainSaTransfer','domainGlobalNew','domainGlobalRenew','domainGlobalTransfer','googleStarter','googleStandard','googlePlus','microsoftBasic','microsoftStandard','microsoftPremium','microsoftCopilot','websitePremium'];
    const prices = {};
    for (const key of priceKeys) {
      const value = Number(content.prices?.[key]);
      if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 0 || value > 100000) return json({ error: `قيمة سعر غير صالحة: ${key}` }, 400);
      prices[key] = value;
    }
    clean.prices = JSON.stringify(prices);
    const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    await env.DB.batch(Object.entries(clean).map(([key, value]) => env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO site_content (content_key, content_value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(content_key) DO UPDATE SET content_value = excluded.content_value, updated_at = excluded.updated_at').bind(key, value, now)));
    return json({ ok: true });
  }

  if (url.pathname === '/api/domain-check' && request.method === 'GET') {
    const domain = (url.searchParams.get('domain') || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!/^(?=.{1,253}$)[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.(com|net|org)$/.test(domain)) {
      return json({ error: 'أدخل اسمًا صالحًا بنهاية .com أو .net أو .org. لفحص .sa أرسل طلبًا وسنتحقق لك.' }, 400);
    }
    try {
      const bootstrapResponse = await fetch('https://data.iana.org/rdap/dns.json', { cf: { cacheTtl: 3600, cacheEverything: true } });
      if (!bootstrapResponse.ok) throw new Error('bootstrap');
      const bootstrap = await bootstrapResponse.json();
      const tld = domain.split('.').pop();
      const service = bootstrap.services.find((item) => item[0].includes(tld));
      const base = service?.[1]?.find((item) => item.startsWith('https://'));
      if (!base) return json({ error: 'تعذر العثور على جهة تحقق لهذا الامتداد حاليًا.' }, 503);
      const result = await fetch(new URL(`domain/${encodeURIComponent(domain)}`, base.endsWith('/') ? base : `${base}/`), { headers: { Accept: 'application/rdap+json, application/json' } });
      if (result.status === 404) return json({ status: 'not-found', message: 'لم يظهر النطاق كمسجل في سجل RDAP. التوفر النهائي يتأكد عند التسجيل؛ فقد توجد قيود أو حجز.' });
      if (result.ok) return json({ status: 'registered', message: 'يظهر النطاق مسجلًا حاليًا في سجل RDAP.' });
      if (result.status === 400) return json({ error: 'تعذر تفسير استجابة جهة النطاق. تحقق من الاسم وحاول لاحقًا.' }, 502);
      return json({ error: 'جهة النطاق لا تستجيب الآن. حاول لاحقًا أو أرسل طلب تحقق.' }, 503);
    } catch {
      return json({ error: 'تعذر التحقق الآن. حاول لاحقًا أو أرسل طلبًا للتحقق اليدوي.' }, 503);
    }
  }

  const statusMatch = url.pathname.match(/^\/api\/order-status\/([^/]+)$/i);
  if (statusMatch && request.method === 'GET') {
    const lookup = decodeURIComponent(statusMatch[1]).trim();
    const uuid = /^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(lookup);
    const shortCode = /^WW-([0-9A-F]{10})$/i.exec(lookup);
    if (!uuid && !shortCode) return json({ error: 'صيغة رقم الطلب غير صحيحة.' }, 400);
    const order = uuid
      ? await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, service, status, created_at FROM customer_orders WHERE id = ?').bind(lookup).first()
      : await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, service, status, created_at FROM customer_orders WHERE upper(substr(replace(id, '-', ''), 1, 10)) = ?").bind(shortCode[1].toUpperCase()).first();
    if (!order) return json({ error: 'لم نعثر على طلب بهذا الرقم.' }, 404);
    return json({ order: { id: order.id, requestNumber: shortRequestNumber(order.id), service: order.service, status: order.status, createdAt: order.created_at } });
  }

  if (url.pathname === '/api/orders' && request.method === 'POST') {
    let body;
    try { body = await readJson(request); }
    catch (error) { return json({ error: error.message }, 400); }
    if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) body = {};
    if (body.termsAccepted !== 'accepted') return json({ error: 'يرجى الموافقة على الشروط والأحكام وسياسة الإلغاء والاسترجاع قبل إرسال الطلب.' }, 400);
    const order = validateOrder(body);
    if (!order) return json({ error: 'يرجى التحقق من الاسم والبريد والجوال والخدمة.' }, 400);
    await env.DB.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO customer_orders (id, name, email, phone, service, message, status, created_at, terms_accepted, terms_version)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'new', ?, 1, ?)
    `).bind(order.id, order.name, order.email, order.phone, order.service, order.message, order.createdAt, order.termsVersion).run();
    return json({ ok: true, orderId: order.id, requestNumber: shortRequestNumber(order.id), message: 'تم استلام طلبك بنجاح.' }, 201);
  }

  if (url.pathname === '/api/session' && request.method === 'POST') return handleLogin(request, env);

  if (url.pathname === '/api/session' && request.method === 'DELETE') {
    if (!isSameOrigin(request)) return json({ error: 'مصدر الطلب غير صالح.' }, 403);
    const token = cookieToken(request);
    if (token) await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM admin_sessions WHERE token_hash = ?').bind(await hash(token)).run();
    return json({ ok: true }, 200, { 'Set-Cookie': sessionCookie('', 0) });
  }

  if (url.pathname === '/api/orders' && request.method === 'GET') {
    if (!await authorized(request, env)) return json({ error: 'سجل الدخول لعرض الطلبات.' }, 401);
    const result = await env.DB.prepare(`
      SELECT id, name, email, phone, service, message, status, terms_accepted AS termsAccepted, terms_version AS termsVersion,
             strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ', created_at, 'unixepoch') AS createdAt
      FROM customer_orders ORDER BY created_at DESC
    `).all();
    const files = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, order_id AS orderId, original_name AS name, size_bytes AS size, created_at AS createdAt FROM customer_files ORDER BY created_at DESC').all();
    const filesByOrder = new Map();
    for (const file of files.results || []) { const group = filesByOrder.get(file.orderId) || []; group.push(file); filesByOrder.set(file.orderId, group); }
    return json({ orders: (result.results || []).map((order) => ({ ...order, requestNumber: shortRequestNumber(order.id), files: filesByOrder.get(order.id) || [] })) });
  }

  if (url.pathname === '/api/upload-invites' && request.method === 'POST') {
    if (!isSameOrigin(request)) return json({ error: 'مصدر الطلب غير صالح.' }, 403);
    if (!await authorized(request, env)) return json({ error: 'سجل الدخول أولًا.' }, 401);
    if (!env.CUSTOMER_FILES) return json({ error: 'فعّل مساحة R2 الخاصة في Cloudflare أولًا.' }, 503);
    let body; try { body = await readJson(request); } catch (error) { return json({ error: error.message }, 400); }
    const orderId = cleanText(body.orderId, 36);
    if (!/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(orderId) || !await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM customer_orders WHERE id = ?').bind(orderId).first()) return json({ error: 'رقم الطلب غير صالح.' }, 400);
    const token = [...crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32))].map((part) => part.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
    const expiresAt = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 7 * 24 * 60 * 60;
    await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO file_upload_invites (token_hash, order_id, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').bind(await hash(token), orderId, expiresAt).run();
    return json({ ok: true, token, expiresAt });
  }

  const uploadInviteMatch = url.pathname.match(/^\/api\/upload-invites\/([a-f0-9]{64})$/i);
  if (uploadInviteMatch && request.method === 'GET') {
    if (!env.CUSTOMER_FILES) return json({ error: 'بوابة رفع الملفات غير مفعلة حاليًا.' }, 503);
    const invite = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT order_id AS orderId, expires_at AS expiresAt, uploaded_count AS uploadedCount FROM file_upload_invites WHERE token_hash = ?').bind(await hash(uploadInviteMatch[1])).first();
    if (!invite || invite.expiresAt <= Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) || invite.uploadedCount >= 5) return json({ error: 'رابط الرفع غير صالح أو انتهت صلاحيته.' }, 404);
    return json({ invite: { orderId: invite.orderId, expiresAt: invite.expiresAt, remaining: 5 - invite.uploadedCount } });
  }

  if (uploadInviteMatch && request.method === 'POST') {
    if (!isSameOrigin(request)) return json({ error: 'مصدر الطلب غير صالح.' }, 403);
    if (!env.CUSTOMER_FILES) return json({ error: 'بوابة رفع الملفات غير مفعلة حاليًا.' }, 503);
    const length = Number(request.headers.get('content-length') || 0);
    if (!length || length > 10 * 1024 * 1024 + 100_000) return json({ error: 'حجم الملف يتجاوز الحد الأقصى (10 ميجابايت).' }, 413);
    const tokenHash = await hash(uploadInviteMatch[1]);
    const invite = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT order_id AS orderId, expires_at AS expiresAt, uploaded_count AS uploadedCount FROM file_upload_invites WHERE token_hash = ?').bind(tokenHash).first();
    if (!invite || invite.expiresAt <= Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) || invite.uploadedCount >= 5) return json({ error: 'رابط الرفع غير صالح أو انتهت صلاحيته.' }, 404);
    let form; try { form = await request.formData(); } catch { return json({ error: 'تعذر قراءة الملف.' }, 400); }
    const file = form.get('file');
    if (!(file instanceof File) || file.size < 1 || file.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) return json({ error: 'اختر ملفًا لا يتجاوز 10 ميجابايت.' }, 400);
    const extensions = { 'application/pdf':'pdf', 'image/png':'png', 'image/jpeg':'jpg', 'image/webp':'webp' };
    const extension = extensions[file.type];
    if (!extension) return json({ error: 'الصيغ المتاحة: PDF وPNG وJPG وWebP.' }, 415);
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(await file.slice(0, 12).arrayBuffer());
    const signatures = { pdf: bytes[0]===37&&bytes[1]===80&&bytes[2]===68&&bytes[3]===70, png: bytes[0]===137&&bytes[1]===80&&bytes[2]===78&&bytes[3]===71, jpg: bytes[0]===255&&bytes[1]===216&&bytes[2]===255, webp: bytes[0]===82&&bytes[1]===73&&bytes[2]===70&&bytes[3]===70&&bytes[8]===87&&bytes[9]===69&&bytes[10]===66&&bytes[11]===80 };
    if (!signatures[extension]) return json({ error: 'محتوى الملف لا يطابق صيغته.' }, 415);
    const id = crypto.randomUUID(); const key = `${invite.orderId}/${id}.${extension}`; const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const quota = await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE file_upload_invites SET uploaded_count = uploaded_count + 1 WHERE token_hash = ? AND expires_at > ? AND uploaded_count < 5').bind(tokenHash, now).run();
    if (!quota.meta?.changes) return json({ error: 'انتهى رابط الرفع أو وصل إلى الحد الأقصى للملفات.' }, 409);
    try { await env.CUSTOMER_FILES.put(key, file.stream(), { httpMetadata: { contentType: 'application/octet-stream' } }); }
    catch { await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE file_upload_invites SET uploaded_count = uploaded_count - 1 WHERE token_hash = ?').bind(tokenHash).run(); return json({ error: 'تعذر تخزين الملف حاليًا.' }, 503); }
    try {
      await env.DB.batch([
        env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO customer_files (id, order_id, object_key, original_name, media_type, size_bytes, created_at, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)').bind(id, invite.orderId, key, cleanText(file.name, 180) || `upload.${extension}`, file.type, file.size, now, now + 60 * 24 * 60 * 60),
      ]);
    } catch { await env.CUSTOMER_FILES.delete(key); await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE file_upload_invites SET uploaded_count = uploaded_count - 1 WHERE token_hash = ?').bind(tokenHash).run(); throw new Error('تعذر حفظ بيانات الملف.'); }
    return json({ ok: true, message: 'تم رفع الملف بأمان.' }, 201);
  }

  const customerFileMatch = url.pathname.match(/^\/api\/customer-files\/([0-9a-f-]{36})$/i);
  if (customerFileMatch && request.method === 'GET') {
    if (!await authorized(request, env)) return json({ error: 'سجل الدخول أولًا.' }, 401);
    const file = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT object_key AS objectKey, original_name AS name, media_type AS mediaType FROM customer_files WHERE id = ?').bind(customerFileMatch[1]).first();
    if (!file) return json({ error: 'الملف غير موجود.' }, 404);
    const object = await env.CUSTOMER_FILES.get(file.objectKey);
    if (!object) return json({ error: 'الملف غير موجود في التخزين.' }, 404);
    const filename = encodeURIComponent(file.name).replace(/['()*]/g, (character) => `%${character.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).toUpperCase()}`);
    return new Response(object.body, { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream', 'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename="file-${customerFileMatch[1]}.${file.name.split('.').pop().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi,'')}"; filename*=UTF-8''${filename}`, 'Cache-Control': 'private, no-store', 'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff' } });
  }

  const statusAdminMatch = url.pathname.match(/^\/api\/orders\/([0-9a-f-]{36})\/status$/i);
  if (statusAdminMatch && request.method === 'PATCH') {
    if (!isSameOrigin(request)) return json({ error: 'مصدر الطلب غير صالح.' }, 403);
    if (!await authorized(request, env)) return json({ error: 'سجل الدخول أولًا.' }, 401);
    let body;
    try { body = await readJson(request); } catch (error) { return json({ error: error.message }, 400); }
    const statuses = new Set(['new', 'reviewing', 'awaiting_customer', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'cancelled']);
    if (!statuses.has(body.status)) return json({ error: 'حالة الطلب غير صالحة.' }, 400);
    const result = await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE customer_orders SET status = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(body.status, statusAdminMatch[1]).run();
    if (!result.meta?.changes) return json({ error: 'الطلب غير موجود.' }, 404);
    return json({ ok: true });
  }

  const deleteMatch = url.pathname.match(/^\/api\/orders\/([^/]+)$/);
  if (deleteMatch && request.method === 'DELETE') {
    if (!isSameOrigin(request)) return json({ error: 'مصدر الطلب غير صالح.' }, 403);
    if (!await authorized(request, env)) return json({ error: 'سجل الدخول أولًا.' }, 401);
    const orderId = decodeURIComponent(deleteMatch[1]);
    const files = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT object_key AS objectKey FROM customer_files WHERE order_id = ?').bind(orderId).all();
    if (env.CUSTOMER_FILES) for (const file of files.results || []) await env.CUSTOMER_FILES.delete(file.objectKey);
    const result = await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM customer_orders WHERE id = ?').bind(orderId).run();
    if (!result.meta?.changes) return json({ error: 'الطلب غير موجود.' }, 404);
    return json({ ok: true });
  }

  if (url.pathname.startsWith('/api/')) return json({ error: 'المسار غير موجود.' }, 404);
  return env.ASSETS.fetch(request);
}

export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    try { return await handleRequest(request, env); }
    catch {
      return json({ error: 'حدث خطأ في الخادم.' }, 500);
    }
  },
  async scheduled(_event, env, ctx) {
    ctx.waitUntil((async () => {
      if (!env.CUSTOMER_FILES) return;
      const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
      const expired = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, object_key AS objectKey FROM customer_files WHERE expires_at <= ?').bind(now).all();
      for (const file of expired.results || []) await env.CUSTOMER_FILES.delete(file.objectKey);
      await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM customer_files WHERE expires_at <= ?').bind(now).run();
      await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM file_upload_invites WHERE expires_at <= ?').bind(now).run();
    })());
  }
};
```

## dns-readiness.html

```html
<!doctype html><html lang="ar" dir="rtl"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><meta name="description" content="قائمة تحقق لإعداد DNS والبريد الاحترافي قبل الإطلاق."><title>جاهزية DNS والبريد | WW</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="styles.css"><link rel="stylesheet" href="service-pages.css"></head><body><header class="site-header"><div class="container nav-wrap no-toggle"><a class="brand" href="index.html"><span class="brand-mark"><svg viewBox="0 0 64 52"><path d="M4 13 10 39 17 22 24 39 31 13M33 13 39 39 46 22 53 39 60 13"/></svg></span><span class="brand-name"><strong>WW</strong><small>DIGITAL SERVICES</small></span></a><nav class="main-nav"><a href="index.html">الرئيسية</a><a href="business-email.html">البريد</a><a href="en/dns-readiness.html" lang="en">English</a></nav></div></header><main><section class="inner-hero"><div class="container"><div class="eyebrow">قائمة تحقق للبريد</div><h1>راجع سجلات النطاق قبل تشغيل البريد.</h1><p>هذه قائمة إرشادية. خذ القيم الفعلية من مزود بريدك، ولا تنسخ سجلات عامة دون الرجوع إلى إعدادات نطاقك.</p></div></section><section class="page-content"><div class="container"><div class="tool-card checklist-items" id="dns-list"><label><input type="checkbox"> أضفت سجلات MX التي حددها مزود البريد.</label><label><input type="checkbox"> أعددت SPF لتحديد مصادر الإرسال المصرح بها.</label><label><input type="checkbox"> فعّلت DKIM من لوحة مزود البريد وأضفت السجل المطلوب.</label><label><input type="checkbox"> أعددت سياسة DMARC مناسبة وراجعت التقارير.</label><label><input type="checkbox"> اختبرت الإرسال والاستقبال من وإلى أكثر من مزود.</label><label><input type="checkbox"> حفظت نسخة من سجلات DNS القديمة قبل التعديل.</label><div class="page-actions"><button class="pill-link" id="print-checklist" type="button">طباعة / حفظ PDF</button><a class="pill-link secondary" href="knowledge.html">شرح المصطلحات</a></div><p class="form-hint">القائمة تحفظ على هذا الجهاز فقط. العلامات لا تفحص DNS تلقائيًا.</p></div></div></section></main><script>const key='ww-dns-readiness';const boxes=[...document.querySelectorAll('#dns-list input')];try{const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');boxes.forEach((b,i)=>b.checked=Boolean(saved[i]));}catch{}boxes.forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('change',()=>localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(boxes.map(x=>x.checked)))));document.querySelector('#print-checklist').addEventListener('click',()=>print());</script></body></html>
```

class="inner-hero"><div class="container"><div class="eyebrow">دليل النطاقات</div><h1>اختر امتدادًا يناسب جمهورك ونشاطك.</h1><p>الامتداد يحدد جزءًا من عنوان موقعك. القرار يعتمد على السوق المستهدف وتوفر الاسم ومتطلبات السجل.</p></div></section><section class="page-content"><div class="container"><div class="comparison-wrap" tabindex="0"><table class="comparison-table"><thead><tr><th>الامتداد</th><th>مناسب عادةً لـ</th><th>السعر المنشور</th><th>ملاحظات</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><th scope="row">.sa</th><td>الأنشطة والجمهور داخل السعودية</td><td>حسب التسجيل أو التجديد؛ يبدأ من 100 ريال</td><td>قد يتطلب مستندات تحقق من أهلية التسجيل.</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">.com</th><td>الأنشطة التجارية والجمهور الدولي</td><td>75 ريال للتسجيل والتجديد</td><td>التوفر النهائي يؤكده وكيل التسجيل.</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">.net</th><td>الخدمات التقنية والشبكات، واستخدامات عامة</td><td>75 ريال للتسجيل والتجديد</td><td>متاح ضمن فحص التوفر الأولي.</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">.org</th><td>المنظمات والمجتمعات والمبادرات</td><td>75 ريال للتسجيل والتجديد</td><td>تحقق من ملاءمة الاسم لنشاطك.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="detail-grid"><article class="detail-card"><h2>نقل النطاق</h2><p>عادةً تحتاج إلى فك قفل النطاق وطلب رمز النقل (Auth/EPP) من الوكيل الحالي، والتأكد من صلاحية البريد المسجل. تختلف الشروط حسب الامتداد والوكيل.</p></article><article class="detail-card"><h2>التجديد قبل الانتهاء</h2><p>راجع تاريخ الانتهاء والوكيل المسؤول وبيانات دخولك. قد تختلف فترة السماح والاستعادة والتكلفة بعد انتهاء النطاق، لذلك ابدأ مبكرًا.</p></article><article class="detail-card"><h2>قبل تغيير DNS</h2><p>احتفظ بنسخة من سجلات DNS الحالية، خصوصًا سجلات البريد MX وSPF وDKIM وDMARC؛ أي تغيير غير مكتمل قد يوقف الموقع أو البريد مؤقتًا.</p></article></div><div class="page-actions"><a class="pill-link" href="index.html?flow=quote&recommendation=إنشاء نطاق#contact">اطلب مساعدة بالنطاق</a><a class="pill-link secondary" href="domains.html">افحص نطاقًا عالميًا</a></div><div class="detail-card"><h2>أسئلة شائعة عن النطاقات</h2><details><summary>هل نتيجة الفحص تعني أن النطاق محجوز لي؟</summary><p>لا. الفحص الأولي لا يحجز الاسم؛ يؤكد وكيل التسجيل التوفر عند تقديم الطلب.</p></details><details><summary>هل نقل النطاق يغير محتوى موقعي؟</summary><p>نقل التسجيل وحده لا ينقل ملفات الموقع. راجع إعدادات DNS والاستضافة قبل تغييرها.</p></details><details><summary>هل يمكن ضمان سعر التجديد لسنوات؟</summary><p>السعر المعروض هو القائمة الحالية، وقد تتغير أسعار السجل أو الوكيل. يؤكد السعر قبل تنفيذ المعاملة.</p></details></div></div></section></main></body></html>

النطاق وتكاليفه النهائية خاضعة للسجل أو وكيل التسجيل.</p><div class="page-actions"><a class="pill-link" href="#domain-check">تحقق من نطاق عالمي</a><a class="pill-link secondary" href="products.html#domains">عرض أسعار النطاقات</a></div></div></section><section class="page-content"><div class="container"><div class="detail-grid"><article class="detail-card"><h2>التسجيل</h2><p>سجل الاسم المناسب لنشاطك بامتدادات سعودية أو عالمية، مع توضيح مدة التسجيل والمتطلبات قبل التنفيذ.</p></article><article class="detail-card"><h2>التجديد</h2><p>اطلب تجديد نطاقك مع متابعة بياناته وتاريخ انتهائه. تعتمد إمكانية التجديد والرسوم على حالة النطاق وامتداده.</p></article><article class="detail-card"><h2>النقل وإعداد DNS</h2><p>نرتب طلب النقل بين الوكلاء ونساعدك في ربط النطاق بالموقع والبريد والخدمات التي تحتاجها.</p></article></div><div class="tool-card" id="domain-check"><h2>فحص أولي للنطاقات العالمية</h2><p>يدعم الفحص .com و.net و.org عبر سجل RDAP. لا يشمل نطاقات .sa، ولا يمثل تأكيدًا نهائيًا لإمكانية التسجيل.</p><form id="domain-form"><input name="domain" type="text" placeholder="مثال: yourbrand.com" aria-label="اسم النطاق" required dir="ltr" autocomplete="url"><button class="pill-link" type="submit">افحص النطاق</button></form><div id="domain-result" class="tool-result" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div></div><h2>كيف نعمل؟</h2><ol class="steps-list"><li>تشاركنا الاسم والامتداد والنشاط.</li><li>نراجع نتيجة التوفر والمتطلبات مع جهة التسجيل.</li><li>ننهي التسجيل أو النقل وننسق ربطه بخدماتك.</li></ol><div class="page-actions"><a class="pill-link" href="index.html#contact?service=domain">اطلب تسجيل نطاق</a></div></div></section></main><a class="pill-link sticky-cta" href="index.html#contact">تواصل معنا ←</a><script>document.querySelector('#domain-form').addEventListener('submit',async e=>{e.preventDefault();const input=e.currentTarget.elements.domain;const result=document.querySelector('#domain-result');const domain=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();result.className='tool-result';result.textContent='جارٍ التحقق…';try{const response=await fetch('/api/domain-check?domain='+encodeURIComponent(domain));const data=await response.json();if(!response.ok)throw new Error(data.error||'تعذر إكمال الفحص');result.textContent=data.message+(data.status==='not-found'?' يمكنك إرسال طلب لتأكيد التسجيل.':'');}catch(error){result.classList.add('error');result.textContent=error.message;}});</script><a ## en/dns-readiness.html

```html
<!doctype html><html lang="en" dir="ltr"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><meta name="description" content="A DNS and email readiness checklist for launching business email."><title>DNS and Email Readiness | WW</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="../styles.css"><link rel="stylesheet" href="../service-pages.css"><style>body{text-align:left}.brand-name small{text-align:left}</style></head><body><header class="site-header"><div class="container nav-wrap no-toggle"><a class="brand" href="index.html"><span class="brand-mark"><svg viewBox="0 0 64 52"><path d="M4 13 10 39 17 22 24 39 31 13M33 13 39 39 46 22 53 39 60 13"/></svg></span><span class="brand-name"><strong>WW</strong><small>DIGITAL SERVICES</small></span></a><nav class="main-nav"><a href="index.html">Home</a><a href="business-email.html">Email</a><a href="../dns-readiness.html" lang="ar">العربية</a></nav></div></header><main><section class="inner-hero"><div class="container"><div class="eyebrow">EMAIL CHECKLIST</div><h1>Review domain records before activating email.</h1><p>This is a general checklist. Use values supplied by your email provider; do not copy generic records without checking your domain setup.</p></div></section><section class="page-content"><div class="container"><div class="tool-card checklist-items" id="dns-list"><label><input type="checkbox"> Add the MX records specified by your email provider.</label><label><input type="checkbox"> Configure SPF to identify authorized sending services.</label><label><input type="checkbox"> Enable DKIM in your email admin console and add its record.</label><label><input type="checkbox"> Configure a suitable DMARC policy and review reports.</label><label><input type="checkbox"> Test sending and receiving across providers.</label><label><input type="checkbox"> Save existing DNS records before making changes.</label><div class="page-actions"><button class="pill-link" id="print-checklist" type="button">Print / save PDF</button><a class="pill-link secondary" href="knowledge.html">Read the glossary</a></div><p class="form-hint">Checklist state is stored on this device. It does not automatically inspect DNS.</p></div></div></section></main><script>const key='ww-dns-readiness';const boxes=[...document.querySelectorAll('#dns-list input')];try{const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');boxes.forEach((b,i)=>b.checked=Boolean(saved[i]));}catch{}boxes.forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('change',()=>localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(boxes.map(x=>x.checked)))));document.querySelector('#print-checklist').addEventListener('click',()=>print());</script></body></html>
```

## en/domain-guide.html

```html
<!doctype html><html lang="en" dir="ltr"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><meta name="description" content="A practical guide to domain extensions, transfers and renewals from WW."><meta name="theme-color" content="#151815"><title>Domain and Transfer Guide | WW</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="../styles.css"><link rel="stylesheet" href="../service-pages.css"><style>body{text-align:left}.brand-name small{text-align:left}</style></head><body><header class="site-header"><div class="container nav-wrap no-toggle"><a class="brand" href="index.html"><span class="brand-mark"><svg viewBox="0 0 64 52"><path d="M4 13 10 39 17 22 24 39 31 13M33 13 39 39 46 22 53 39 60 13"/></svg></span><span class="brand-name"><strong>WW</strong><small>DIGITAL SERVICES</small></span></a><nav class="main-nav"><a href="index.html">Home</a><a href="domains.html">Domains</a><a href="products.html">Pricing</a><a href="../domain-guide.html" lang="ar">العربية</a></nav></div></header><main><section class="inner-hero"><div class="container"><div class="eyebrow">DOMAIN GUIDE</div><h1>Choose an extension for your audience and business.</h1><p>The right extension depends on your market, name availability and registry requirements.</p></div></section><section class="page-content"><div class="container"><div class="comparison-wrap" tabindex="0"><table class="comparison-table"><thead><tr><th>Extension</th><th>Common fit</th><th>Published price</th><th>Notes</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><th scope="row">.sa</th><td>Businesses and audiences in Saudi Arabia</td><td>From SAR 100 for registration or renewal</td><td>Eligibility documents may be required.</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">.com</th><td>Commercial and international audiences</td><td>SAR 75 registration and renewal</td><td>Registrar confirms final availability.</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">.net</th><td>Technology and network services; general use</td><td>SAR 75 registration and renewal</td><td>Included in the initial availability check.</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">.org</th><td>Organizations, communities and initiatives</td><td>SAR 75 registration and renewal</td><td>Check that it suits your activity.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="detail-grid"><article class="detail-card"><h2>Transferring a domain</h2><p>You may need to unlock the domain, request its Auth/EPP code from your current registrar and confirm access to the registrant email. Rules vary by extension and registrar.</p></article><article class="detail-card"><h2>Renew before expiry</h2><p>Check the expiry date, registrar and account access. Grace and restoration periods and fees vary, so start early.</p></article><article class="detail-card"><h2>Before changing DNS</h2><p>Keep a copy of existing DNS records, especially MX, SPF, DKIM and DMARC. An incomplete change can interrupt your website or email.</p></article></div><div class="page-actions"><a class="pill-link" href="index.html?flow=quote&recommendation=%D8%A5%D9%86%D8%B4%D8%A7%D8%A1%20%D9%86%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%82#contact">Ask for domain help</a><a class="pill-link secondary" href="domains.html">Check a global domain</a></div><div class="detail-card"><h2>Domain FAQs</h2><details><summary>Does an availability result reserve the domain?</summary><p>No. The initial check does not reserve a name; the registrar confirms availability during ordering.</p></details><details><summary>Will a domain transfer move my website?</summary><p>A registration transfer alone does not move website files. Review DNS and hosting before making changes.</p></details><details><summary>Can renewal prices be guaranteed for years?</summary><p>Published prices reflect the current list and may change. The final price is confirmed before processing.</p></details></div></div></section></main></body></html>
```

## en/domains.html

```html
﻿<!doctype html><html lang="en" dir="ltr"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><meta name="description" content="Domain registration, renewal, transfer and DNS setup for Saudi and global domains with WW."><title>Domain Services | WW</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="../styles.css"><link rel="stylesheet" href="../service-pages.css"><link rel="alternate" hreflang="ar" href="../domains.html"><style>body{text-align:left}.brand-name small{text-align:left}</style></head><body><header class="site-header"><div class="container nav-wrap no-toggle"><a class="brand" href="index.html"><span class="brand-mark"><svg viewBox="0 0 64 52"><path d="M4 13 10 39 17 22 24 39 31 13M33 13 39 39 46 22 53 39 60 13"/></svg></span><span class="brand-name"><strong>WW</strong><small>DIGITAL SERVICES</small></span></a><nav class="main-nav"><a href="index.html">Home</a><a href="products.html">Pricing</a><a href="portfolio.html">Concepts</a><a href="track-order.html">Track request</a><a href="../domains.html" lang="ar">العربية</a></nav><a class="button button-small nav-cta" href="index.html#contact">Get started</a></div></header><main><section class="inner-hero"><div class="container"><div class="eyebrow">01 / DOMAINS</div><h1>Your digital address starts with a name people remember.</h1><p>We help you choose a domain that fits your business and coordinate registration, renewal or transfer. Availability and final charges are confirmed by the relevant registry or registrar.</p><div class="page-actions"><a class="pill-link" href="#domain-check">Check a global domain</a><a class="pill-link secondary" href="products.html#domains">View domain prices</a></div></div></section><section class="page-content"><div class="container"><div class="detail-grid"><article class="detail-card"><h2>Registration</h2><p>Register a suitable Saudi or global domain, with the term and eligibility requirements clarified before submission.</p></article><article class="detail-card"><h2>Renewal</h2><p>Request renewal and get help reviewing the domain status and renewal timing. Eligibility and fees vary by extension and status.</p></article><article class="detail-card"><h2>Transfer and DNS</h2><p>Coordinate an inter-registrar transfer and connect your domain to your website, email and other services.</p></article></div><div class="tool-card" id="domain-check"><h2>Initial check for global domains</h2><p>This tool checks .com, .net and .org through RDAP. It does not cover .sa and does not guarantee that a domain can be registered. A registrar confirms final availability.</p><form id="domain-form"><input name="domain" type="text" placeholder="yourbrand.com" aria-label="Domain name" required dir="ltr" autocomplete="url"><button class="pill-link" type="submit">Check domain</button></form><div id="domain-result" class="tool-result" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div></div><h2>How it works</h2><ol class="steps-list"><li>Share the name, extension and business activity.</li><li>We review the preliminary result and registrar requirements.</li><li>We coordinate registration or transfer and connect your services.</li></ol><div class="page-actions"><a class="pill-link" href="index.html#contact">Request domain service</a><a class="pill-link secondary" href="index.html?flow=reminder#contact">Request a manual expiry follow-up</a></div></div></section></main><a class="pill-link sticky-cta" href="index.html#contact">Contact us →</a><script>document.querySelector('#domain-form').addEventListener('submit',async e=>{e.preventDefault();const input=e.currentTarget.elements.domain;const result=document.querySelector('#domain-result');result.className='tool-result';result.textContent='Checking…';try{const response=await fetch('/api/domain-check?domain='+encodeURIComponent(input.value.trim().toLowerCase()));const data=await response.json();if(!response.ok)throw new Error('The check could not be completed. Please use a .com, .net or .org name, or contact us to check a .sa domain.');result.textContent=data.status==='not-found'?'The domain does not appear as registered in the RDAP directory. This is not final confirmation; the registrar must confirm availability.':data.status==='registered'?'The domain currently appears as registered in the RDAP directory.':'The check could not be completed. Please try again or contact us.';}catch(error){result.classList.add('error');result.textContent=error.message;}});</script><a class="whatsapp-float" href="https://wa.me/96650210929" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="Open a WhatsApp chat with WW" title="Open a WhatsApp chat with WW"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20.3 11.8a8.3 8.3 0 0 1-12.1 7.4L3 20.5l1.3-5A8.3 8.3 0 1 1 20.3 11.8Z"/><path d="M8.1 7.7c.2-.4.4-.4.7-.4h.5c.2 0 .4.1.5.4l.8 1.8c.1.2.1.4-.1.6l-.6.7c-.2.2-.2.4 0 .7.7 1.2 1.6 2.1 2.8 2.7.3.1.5.1.7-.1l.8-.9c.2-.2.4-.2.6-.1l1.8.9c.2.1.3.3.3.5 0 .5-.3 1.2-.8 1.6-.5.5-1.4.8-2.1.7-1.2-.2-2.7-.9-4.2-2.2-1.8-1.6-2.8-3.6-2.9-4.7-.1-.8.3-1.7.8-2.2.3-.2.6-.3 1-.3Z"/></svg><span>WhatsApp<small>+966 50 210 0929</small></span></a></body></html>
```

## en/file-upload.html

```html
<!doctype html><html lang="en" dir="ltr"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><meta name="robots" content="noindex,nofollow"><meta name="referrer" content="no-referrer"><meta name="description" content="Upload project files privately through a time-limited link."><title>Upload Project Files | WW</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="../styles.css"><link rel="stylesheet" href="../service-pages.css"><style>body{text-align:left}.brand-name small{text-align:left}</style></head><body><header class="site-header"><div class="container nav-wrap no-toggle"><a class="brand" href="index.html"><span class="brand-mark"><svg viewBox="0 0 64 52"><path d="M4 13 10 39 17 22 24 39 31 13M33 13 39 39 46 22 53 39 60 13"/></svg></span><span class="brand-name"><strong>WW</strong><small>DIGITAL SERVICES</small></span></a><nav class="main-nav"><a href="index.html">Home</a><a href="client-files.html">File information</a><a href="../file-upload.html" lang="ar">العربية</a></nav></div></header><main><section class="inner-hero"><div class="container"><div class="eyebrow">PRIVATE UPLOAD LINK</div><h1>Upload your project files.</h1><p>This link is for one request and expires after seven days. PDF, PNG, JPG and WebP are accepted, up to 10 MB per file and five files. Files are automatically deleted after 60 days.</p></div></section><section class="page-content"><div class="container"><form id="upload-form" class="tool-card"><label for="file-input">Choose a file</label><input id="file-input" name="file" type="file" accept="application/pdf,image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp" required><p id="upload-state" role="status" aria-live="polite">Checking upload link…</p><button class="pill-link" type="submit" disabled>Upload file</button></form></div></section></main><script>const token=new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('token')||'';const form=document.querySelector('#upload-form');const state=document.querySelector('#upload-state');const button=form.querySelector('button');let remaining=0;fetch('/api/upload-invites/'+encodeURIComponent(token)).then(async r=>{const d=await r.json();if(!r.ok)throw Error(d.error||'Invalid upload link.');remaining=d.invite.remaining;state.textContent=`Link is valid for request ${d.invite.orderId}. You can upload ${remaining} file(s).`;button.disabled=false;}).catch(e=>state.textContent=e.message);form.addEventListener('submit',async e=>{e.preventDefault();if(!token||!form.reportValidity())return;button.disabled=true;state.textContent='Uploading file…';try{const data=new FormData(form);const r=await fetch('/api/upload-invites/'+encodeURIComponent(token),{method:'POST',body:data});const d=await r.json();if(!r.ok)throw Error(d.error||'Upload failed.');remaining--;form.reset();state.textContent=remaining?`${d.message} You can upload ${remaining} more file(s).`:d.message;}catch(error){state.textContent=error.message;}finally{button.disabled=remaining<=0;}});</script></body></html>
```

```html
    <section class="about section-pad" id="about"><div class="container about-layout"><div class="about-visual"><div class="about-mark">WW<span>✳</span></div><div class="about-caption">DIGITAL SERVICES / SAUDI ARABIA</div></div><div class="about-copy"><div class="eyebrow dark-eyebrow"><span>02</span> ABOUT WW</div><h2>Clear digital foundations,<br><span>without the guesswork.</span></h2><p>WW helps businesses bring their domain, website and business email together. We focus on clear communication, a defined scope and practical setup so you know what happens at every step.</p><div class="values"><div><b>Clarity</b><span>Understand each step</span></div><div><b>Care</b><span>Solutions for your needs</span></div><div><b>Continuity</b><span>A foundation that can grow</span></div></div><a class="text-link" href="terms.html">Read our service terms →</a></div></div></section>
    <section class="steps section-pad" id="steps"><div class="container"><div class="steps-heading"><div class="eyebrow dark-eyebrow"><span>03</span> HOW IT WORKS</div><h2>Three clear steps,<br><span>from idea to launch.</span></h2></div><div class="steps-grid"><article><span>01</span><div class="step-icon">⌕</div><h3>Tell us what you need</h3><p>We learn about your business and goals, then identify suitable services.</p></article><article><span>02</span><div class="step-icon">✳</div><h3>We prepare your setup</h3><p>We coordinate your domain, website and email according to the agreed scope.</p></article><article><span>03</span><div class="step-icon">↗</div><h3>Launch with confidence</h3><p>We help prepare your digital foundation so you can connect with customers.</p></article></div></div></section>
    <section class="section-pad"><div class="container"><div class="section-head"><div><div class="eyebrow dark-eyebrow"><span>04</span> FAQ</div><h2>Answers before<br><span>you get started.</span></h2></div><p>Quick information about our services and the next steps.</p></div><div class="faq-list"><details><summary>Are the published prices final?</summary><p>Published prices are a guide based on the current price list. A written quote confirms the final price, term, scope and any recurring charges before work begins.</p></details><details><summary>Will you set up and connect business email?</summary><p>Yes. We create the email accounts and connect the service to your domain. The service is ready to use once provider activation is complete and the required details are available.</p></details><details><summary>Does the domain checker guarantee availability?</summary><p>The initial check supports .com, .net and .org. The registrar confirms final availability. Saudi .sa domains require a separate check.</p></details><details><summary>What should I prepare for a website project?</summary><p>Share your business description, services and audience, plus any text, branding and images you have permission to use. The written scope will define the deliverables.</p></details><details><summary>How can I track my request?</summary><p>Keep the request number shown after submission and enter it on our request-tracking page.</p></details></div><div class="resource-cards" aria-label="Site tools and resources"><a class="detail-card" href="service-finder.html"><b>Service finder</b><span>Answer a few questions to find a starting point.</span></a><a class="detail-card" href="plan-comparison.html"><b>Compare email plans</b><span>Review storage, features and annual pricing.</span></a><a class="detail-card" href="website-checklist.html"><b>Website checklist</b><span>Organize content and materials before implementation.</span></a><a class="detail-card" href="knowledge.html"><b>Knowledge center</b><span>Guides, definitions and service-specific FAQs.</span></a><a class="detail-card" href="aftercare.html"><b>After-handover guide</b><span>Practical tips for your domain, website and email.</span></a><a class="detail-card" href="about.html"><b>About WW</b><span>How we work and what we provide.</span></a><a class="detail-card" href="index.html?flow=appointment#contact"><b>Suggest a consultation time</b><span>Send a preferred time and we will confirm it with you.</span></a></div><div class="page-actions"><a class="button" href="quote-calculator.html">Estimate a cost <span>→</span></a><a class="text-link" href="track-order.html">Track a request →</a></div></div></section>
```
