/* Soft, low contrast palette and accessible contact form */
:root{--navy:#314156;--ink:#29384a;--muted:#77818e;--cream:#f6f5f1;--paper:#fbfaf7;--lime:#dce5c8;--line:#e6e8e2;--mint:#e7ebe2}
.about{background:#3d4d5d}
.emblem-logo{color:#dce5c8}
.emblem-logo:after{background:#dce5c8}
.emblem-cross,.text-link span,.mock-button b{color:#dce5c8}
.hero:before{background:radial-gradient(ellipse at 85% 38%,rgba(111,132,133,.22),transparent 35%),linear-gradient(120deg,transparent 56%,rgba(255,255,255,.018) 56%,transparent 56.2%)}
.art-glow{background:radial-gradient(circle,rgba(220,229,200,.09),rgba(130,148,142,.06) 47%,transparent 70%)}
.contact{background:#eef0e9;color:#29384a;padding:90px 0;}
.contact-layout{position:relative;z-index:1;display:grid;grid-template-columns:.85fr 1.15fr;gap:50px;align-items:center}
.contact-intro{position:relative;z-index:1}
.contact-symbol{width:70px;height:70px;margin-bottom:27px;border-color:#cbd1c6;color:#526252}
.contact-symbol:after{background:#9cac8d}
.contact-copy h2,.contact-intro h2{font-size:31px;letter-spacing:-.06em;margin:9px 0;color:#29384a}
.contact-intro>p{font-size:11px;line-height:2.1;color:#707b83;max-width:370px}
.contact-intro .eyebrow{color:#7c8974}
.contact-note{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-top:22px;color:#798479;font-size:9px}
.contact-note span{color:#82916e;font-size:15px}
.contact-form{position:relative;z-index:1;background:#fbfaf7;border:1px solid #e4e7df;border-radius:12px;padding:27px;box-shadow:0 12px 36px #3542520a}
.form-heading{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:20px}
.form-heading h3{font-size:16px;margin:0;color:#29384a}
.form-heading>span{font-size:8px;color:#929a9f}
.form-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:15px}
.contact-form label{display:grid;gap:6px;font-size:10px;font-weight:600;color:#49586a}
.contact-form input,.contact-form select,.contact-form textarea{width:100%;border:1px solid #e0e4dd;background:#fff;border-radius:6px;padding:11px 12px;font:400 10px var(--font);color:#29384a;outline:none;transition:border-color .2s,box-shadow .2s}
.contact-form input,.contact-form select{height:43px}
.contact-form textarea{resize:vertical;min-height:70px;line-height:1.8}
.contact-form input:focus,.contact-form select:focus,.contact-form textarea:focus{border-color:#9cac8d;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px #9cac8d20}
.contact-form input::placeholder,.contact-form textarea::placeholder{color:#a2a9ad}
.contact-form select:invalid{color:#a2a9ad}
.message-label{margin-top:15px}
.message-label>span{font-weight:400;color:#9ba2a4}
.form-submit{margin-top:16px;min-height:46px;border:0;cursor:pointer;background:#dce5c8}
.form-hint{font-size:8px;color:#929a9f;margin:10px 0 0}
.form-result{margin-top:17px;padding:16px;background:#f0f3eb;border:1px solid #e1e8d9;border-radius:8px}
.form-result h4{font-size:13px;margin:0 0 4px;color:#405344}
.form-result p{font-size:9px;line-height:1.9;color:#6c7970;margin:0 0 12px}
.result-actions{display:flex;gap:9px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.result-link{font-size:9px;font-weight:600;color:#fff;background:#75866a;border-radius:5px;padding:9px 13px}
.result-link:hover{background:#647858}
.result-edit{border:0;background:transparent;color:#737e83;font:500 9px var(--font);margin-top:12px;padding:4px 0;cursor:pointer}
.contact-form.submitted .form-grid,.contact-form.submitted .message-label,.contact-form.submitted .form-submit,.contact-form.submitted .form-hint{display:none}
.contact-orb{opacity:.35}
@media(max-width:900px){.contact-layout{grid-template-columns:.8fr 1.2fr;gap:25px}.contact{padding:70px 0}.contact-form{padding:21px}}
@media(max-width:620px){.contact{padding:60px 0}.contact-layout{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:23px}.contact-symbol{width:56px;height:56px;margin-bottom:18px;font-size:19px}.contact-symbol:after{top:14px;right:9px;width:31px;height:3px}.contact-symbol span{top:9px;right:8px;font-size:6px}.contact-intro h2{font-size:25px}.contact-intro>p{font-size:10px;margin:5px 0}.contact-note{margin-top:12px}.contact-form{padding:19px 16px}.form-grid{gap:12px 9px}.contact-form input,.contact-form select{padding:9px;font-size:9px}.form-heading h3{font-size:14px}.form-heading>span{font-size:7px}.result-actions{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}.result-link{text-align:center;padding:9px 5px}}
/* Matte charcoal visual identity */
:root{--navy:#17191a;--ink:#292c2d;--muted:#737879;--cream:#f3f2ee;--paper:#faf9f6;--lime:#d0d0c6;--line:#dedfda;--mint:#e5e5df}
body{background:var(--cream)}
.brand-mark{background:#ffffff08;border:1px solid #ffffff32;border-radius:13px 13px 13px 3px}
.brand-mark svg{fill:none;stroke:var(--lime);stroke-width:4;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round}
.footer-brand .brand-mark{background:#ffffff08}
.button,.form-submit{background:var(--lime);color:#1b1d1d}
.button:hover,.form-submit:hover{background:#e0e0d7}
.hero{background:#131516}
.hero:before{background:radial-gradient(ellipse at 82% 42%,#777d7530,transparent 38%),linear-gradient(120deg,transparent 56%,#ffffff04 56%,transparent 56.2%)}
.live-dot{background:#c5c7bc;box-shadow:0 0 14px #c5c7bc45}
.about{background:#1b1e1f}
.emblem-logo{color:#d0d0c6}
.emblem-logo:after{background:#d0d0c6}
.emblem-cross,.text-link span,.mock-button b{color:#d0d0c6}
.site-footer{background:#111314}
.hero-art{height:478px}
.hero-photo{position:absolute;inset:4px 10px 9px 15px;overflow:hidden;border-radius:18px;margin:0;background:#252829;box-shadow:0 30px 85px #0008;transform:rotate(-2deg)}
.hero-photo img{position:absolute;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;object-position:center;filter:saturate(.55) contrast(1.04);animation:photo-drift 24s ease-in-out infinite alternate}
.photo-shade{position:absolute;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(270deg,#101213d9 0%,#111415b3 39%,#10121229 78%),linear-gradient(0deg,#101212a8,transparent 60%)}
.hero-photo figcaption{position:absolute;inset:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:center;padding:42px 45px;color:#f0f0eb;direction:rtl}
.photo-overline{font:500 8px/1.8 var(--font);letter-spacing:.18em;color:#d2d2c7b8;direction:ltr}
.hero-photo figcaption strong{font-size:clamp(28px,3.5vw,43px);line-height:1.5;letter-spacing:-.07em;margin-top:18px}
.photo-caption-line{border-top:1px solid #ffffff35;margin-top:17px;padding-top:12px;color:#e1e2dad1;font-size:9px}
.floating-tag{background:#212425ed;color:#eee;border:1px solid #ffffff20;box-shadow:0 12px 35px #0007;backdrop-filter:blur(12px)}
.floating-tag small{color:#b0b5b2}
.tag-icon,.mail-icon{background:#3b413b;color:#d0d0c6}
.tag-domain>i{color:#c5c7bc}
.art-stamp{left:7%;top:41px;width:70px;height:70px;background:#cacbc1;color:#1d2020;border:3px solid #f1f0e9;box-shadow:0 7px 30px #0009}
.art-stamp span{font:800 15px/1 var(--font);letter-spacing:-.14em}
.art-stamp>b{font-size:10px;color:#687062}
.art-caption{color:#d0d2cc;bottom:0}
.art-caption span{background:#c9cabe}
.service-grid{gap:17px}
.service-card{padding:0 0 21px;overflow:hidden;border-color:#e5e5df;background:#fff;box-shadow:0 10px 34px #1e20200a}
.service-photo{height:190px;position:relative;overflow:hidden;background:#242829}
.service-photo:after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(0deg,#121516b8,transparent 70%),linear-gradient(90deg,#1013142b,#10131408)}
.service-photo img{width:100%;height:100%;display:block;object-fit:cover;filter:saturate(.58);transition:transform .65s cubic-bezier(.2,.7,.2,1),filter .4s}
.service-card:hover .service-photo img{transform:scale(1.045);filter:saturate(.78)}
.photo-index{position:absolute;z-index:1;right:17px;bottom:14px;color:#ffffffd4;font:500 8px var(--font);letter-spacing:.12em;direction:ltr}
.service-photo .service-icon{position:absolute;z-index:1;top:14px;left:14px;width:34px;height:34px;border:1px solid #ffffff55;background:#11151868;color:#f0f0e9;backdrop-filter:blur(8px)}
.service-card h3,.service-card p,.service-card .service-link{margin-right:21px;margin-left:21px}
.service-card h3{margin-top:17px}
.service-card p{min-height:64px}
.service-link{margin-top:14px}
.section-head h2 span,.steps-heading h2 span{color:#747c73}
.dark-eyebrow>span{color:#747c73}
.service-link{color:#515b54}
.service-link span{color:#4f5751}
.contact{background:#e8e8e2;color:#292c2d}
.contact-form{background:#f8f8f4;border-color:#dadcd5;box-shadow:0 20px 60px #292c2d0c}
.contact-form input,.contact-form select,.contact-form textarea{background:#fff;border-color:#dfe0da}
.contact-form input:focus,.contact-form select:focus,.contact-form textarea:focus{border-color:#8c9387;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px #8c938718}
.form-result{background:#edeee8;border-color:#d7d9d0;color:#434b43}
.form-result.error{background:#f3e8e5;border-color:#e2cbc5;color:#71453f}
.form-result.error::before{content:"تعذر إرسال الطلب";display:block;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:3px}
.form-submit:disabled{opacity:.68;cursor:wait}
.contact-orb{opacity:.2;border-color:#555d5224;box-shadow:0 0 0 28px #ffffff10,0 0 0 56px #ffffff08}
@keyframes photo-drift{from{transform:scale(1.015)}to{transform:scale(1.09)}}
@media(max-width:900px){.hero-photo{inset:4px 8px 8px}.service-photo{height:150px}}
@media(max-width:620px){.hero-photo{inset:7px 8px 13px;border-radius:13px}.hero-photo figcaption{padding:26px}.photo-overline{font-size:6px}.hero-photo figcaption strong{font-size:26px;margin-top:12px}.photo-caption-line{font-size:7px;margin-top:10px;padding-top:8px}.art-stamp{left:2%;top:21px;width:57px;height:57px}.art-stamp span{font-size:12px}.service-photo{height:190px}.service-card p{min-height:unset}.service-card h3,.service-card p,.service-card .service-link{margin-right:18px;margin-left:18px}}
@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){html{scroll-behavior:auto}*,*:before,*:after{animation-duration:.01ms!important;animation-iteration-count:1!important;transition-duration:.01ms!important}}
.emblem-logo{width:172px;height:140px;display:grid;place-items:center;margin:0}
.emblem-logo svg{width:100%;height:100%;fill:none;stroke:var(--lime);stroke-width:3.4;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round}
.emblem-logo:after{display:none}
.contact-symbol{display:grid;place-items:center}
.contact-symbol svg{width:63%;height:auto;fill:none;stroke:#526252;stroke-width:4;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round}
.contact-symbol:after{display:none}
.service-photo img{object-position:center}
.card-domain .service-photo img{object-position:center 50%}
.card-site .service-photo img{object-position:center 47%}
.card-email .service-photo img{object-position:center 46%}
@media(max-width:620px){.emblem-logo{width:128px;height:103px}.contact-symbol svg{width:63%}}

/* Terms and cancellation policies */
.policies{background:#f2f2ed;color:#292c2d}
.policies-heading{display:flex;align-items:end;justify-content:space-between;gap:24px;margin-bottom:28px}
.policies-heading h2{font-size:clamp(26px,3vw,38px);letter-spacing:-.06em;margin:8px 0 0}
.policies-heading>p{max-width:360px;margin:0;color:#727871;font-size:11px}
.policy-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:14px;align-items:start}
.policy-card{background:#fbfbf8;border:1px solid #dedfd8;border-radius:10px;box-shadow:0 10px 30px #22240d08;overflow:hidden}
.policy-card summary{list-style:none;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:13px;padding:18px 20px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:#303632}
.policy-card summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}
.policy-number{font:600 9px/1 var(--font);color:#798474;letter-spacing:.08em}
.policy-toggle{margin-right:auto;width:27px;height:27px;border:1px solid #dfe1d9;border-radius:50%;display:grid;place-items:center;color:#64705f;font-size:18px;font-weight:400;transition:transform .2s}
.policy-card[open] .policy-toggle{transform:rotate(45deg)}
.policy-content{padding:0 20px 19px;color:#626a64;font-size:10px;line-height:2.1}
.policy-content p{margin:0 0 10px}
.policy-content ul{padding:0 17px 0 0;margin:0}
.policy-content li{padding-right:2px;margin:0 0 7px}
.policy-content li::marker{color:#8c987c}
.policy-contact{border-top:1px solid #e8e9e3;padding-top:12px;margin-top:12px!important}
.policy-contact a{color:#59694f;text-decoration:underline;text-underline-offset:3px;font-weight:700}
.footer-meta{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:18px;font-size:9px}
.footer-meta a{color:#d4d9d1;transition:color .2s}
.footer-meta a:hover{color:#e1e4d8}
@media(max-width:800px){.policy-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}.policies-heading{align-items:start;flex-direction:column;gap:9px}.footer-meta{flex-wrap:wrap;gap:9px 14px}}
@media(max-width:620px){.policy-card summary{padding:15px;font-size:11px}.policy-content{padding:0 15px 16px;font-size:9px}.footer-meta{grid-column:1/-1;grid-row:3;justify-content:flex-start}.footer-meta span{width:100%;text-align:right}.footer-wrap>span:last-child{display:none}}
.disclaimer-card{grid-column:1/-1}
.disclaimer-card .policy-content ul{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:0 24px}
@media(max-width:800px){.disclaimer-card .policy-content ul{grid-template-columns:1fr}}

.contact-form .terms-consent{display:flex;grid-template-columns:none;align-items:flex-start;gap:10px;margin:18px 0 15px;color:#666e67;font-size:10px;font-weight:400;line-height:1.9;cursor:pointer}
.contact-form .terms-consent input{appearance:auto;flex:0 0 17px;width:17px;height:17px;padding:0;margin:4px 0 0;accent-color:#68765f;cursor:pointer}
.terms-consent a{color:#54664d;font-weight:700;text-decoration:underline;text-underline-offset:3px}
.terms-consent input:focus-visible{outline:2px solid #77896d;outline-offset:3px}
.quick-contact{position:fixed;z-index:30;bottom:18px;left:18px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:12px 18px;border:1px solid #aab39f;border-radius:999px;background:#303a32;color:#fff;text-decoration:none;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;box-shadow:0 9px 28px #10151155;transition:transform .2s,background .2s}.quick-contact:hover{transform:translateY(-3px);background:#465346}.quick-contact span{font-size:18px}@media(max-width:620px){.quick-contact{bottom:12px;left:12px;padding:10px 15px;font-size:11px}}
.skip-link{position:absolute;z-index:100;top:8px;right:8px;padding:10px 14px;background:#f1f3eb;color:#182019;border-radius:7px;transform:translateY(-160%)}.skip-link:focus{transform:translateY(0)}
@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){*,*::before,*::after{scroll-behavior:auto!important;animation-duration:.01ms!important;animation-iteration-count:1!important;transition-duration:.01ms!important}}

/* Shared readability rules for both Arabic and English pages. */
html{-webkit-text-size-adjust:100%;text-size-adjust:100%}
body :is(h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,p,li,a,button,label,summary,dt,dd,figcaption,blockquote,td,th){letter-spacing:normal!important;word-spacing:normal}
body :is(h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6){line-height:1.5!important;text-wrap:pretty}
body :is(p,li,label,summary,dd,figcaption,blockquote){line-height:1.85!important}
:is(main,footer) :is(h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,p,li,a,label,summary,dt,dd,figcaption,blockquote,td,th){min-width:0;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.section-head h2,.steps-heading h2,.about-copy h2,.contact-copy h2,.contact-intro h2,.policies-heading h2{line-height:1.55}
.faq-list{display:grid;gap:0;margin:26px 0 34px;border-top:1px solid var(--line)}
.faq-list details{padding:18px 4px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
.faq-list summary{cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;line-height:1.9}
.faq-list details p{max-width:850px;margin:12px 0 0;color:var(--muted);font-size:13px;line-height:2}
.resource-cards{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));gap:14px;margin:26px 0}
.resource-cards .detail-card{display:grid;align-content:start;gap:9px;min-width:0;min-height:100%;padding:22px 20px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;background:var(--paper);color:var(--ink);text-decoration:none;transition:transform .2s,border-color .2s,box-shadow .2s}
.resource-cards .detail-card:hover{transform:translateY(-3px);border-color:#a8b39f;box-shadow:0 12px 30px #17243a0a}
.resource-cards b{display:block;font-size:14px;line-height:1.8;overflow-wrap:break-word}
.resource-cards span{display:block;color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;line-height:1.9}
[hidden]{display:none!important}
@media(max-width:900px){.resource-cards{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}}
@media(max-width:620px){.section-head h2,.steps-heading h2{line-height:1.55}.resource-cards{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:10px}.resource-cards .detail-card{padding:18px}.faq-list details{padding:16px 2px}}
/* Add visible gaps between Arabic words while keeping letters within each word connected. */
html[lang="ar"] body{word-spacing:.2em!important}
html[lang="ar"] body :is(h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,p,li,a,button,label,summary,dt,dd,figcaption,blockquote,td,th,small,strong,b,span,.eyebrow){letter-spacing:normal!important;word-spacing:.2em!important}
html[lang="ar"] .eyebrow,html[lang="ar"] .faq-list summary,html[lang="ar"] .resource-cards b,html[lang="ar"] .resource-cards span{letter-spacing:normal!important;word-spacing:.25em!important;line-height:1.9}
html[lang="ar"] .dark-eyebrow{font-size:13px;line-height:2.1;letter-spacing:normal!important;word-spacing:.25em!important}
html[lang="ar"] .dark-eyebrow>span{font-size:10px;letter-spacing:normal!important}
html[lang="ar"] .faq-list summary{font-size:15px;line-height:2;letter-spacing:normal!important;word-spacing:.25em!important}
html[lang="ar"] .resource-cards b{font-size:15px;line-height:1.9;letter-spacing:normal!important;word-spacing:.25em!important}
.whatsapp-float{position:fixed;z-index:35;right:18px;bottom:18px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 16px;border:1px solid #68ae88;border-radius:999px;background:#167a50;color:#fff;text-decoration:none;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;box-shadow:0 10px 30px #07140e55;transition:transform .2s,background .2s}.whatsapp-float:hover{transform:translateY(-3px);background:#126743}.whatsapp-float svg{width:24px;height:24px;flex:none;fill:none;stroke:currentColor;stroke-width:1.7;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round}.whatsapp-float span{display:grid;line-height:1.25}.whatsapp-float small{font:500 9px/1.5 var(--font);opacity:.83;direction:ltr;text-align:inherit}@media(max-width:620px){.whatsapp-float{right:12px;bottom:12px;padding:9px 12px;font-size:11px}.whatsapp-float svg{width:22px;height:22px}.whatsapp-float small{font-size:8px}}
.request-wizard{display:grid;gap:18px;margin-top:18px}.wizard-progress{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:8px;list-style:none;padding:0;margin:0}.wizard-progress li{padding:9px 10px;border:1px solid #e2e4dc;border-radius:8px;color:#818981;font-size:9px;line-height:1.6}.wizard-progress li.current{background:#e9eee1;color:#3e4c3d;border-color:#b8c4ae;font-weight:700}.wizard-panel{padding:3px 0}.wizard-panel[hidden]{display:none}.wizard-controls{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px}.wizard-controls .button{min-height:40px}.wizard-back{background:#edf0e8}.wizard-summary{white-space:pre-line;background:#f5f6f2;border:1px solid #e3e5df;border-radius:8px;padding:14px;color:#475247;font-size:10px;line-height:2}.wizard-panel h4{margin:0 0 10px;font-size:14px}.wizard-live{min-height:1em;margin:0;color:#778274;font-size:9px}.conditional-fields[hidden]{display:none}.conditional-fields:not([hidden]){margin-bottom:14px}.contact-form .extra-consent{grid-column:1/-1;margin:0}.conditional-note{grid-column:1/-1;margin:0}.demo-tabs{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:18px 0}.demo-tabs button{border:1px solid #d9ddd4;border-radius:50px;padding:9px 14px;background:#f4f5f0;color:#414b40;cursor:pointer}.demo-tabs button[aria-pressed=true]{background:#354239;color:white}.interactive-demo{min-height:300px;padding:26px;border-radius:16px;background:linear-gradient(145deg,#15231d,#526d57);color:#fff;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr .8fr;align-items:center;gap:20px;overflow:hidden}.interactive-demo[data-theme=studio]{background:linear-gradient(145deg,#22252c,#6b7077)}.interactive-demo[data-theme=consult]{background:linear-gradient(145deg,#30291f,#86704e)}.demo-copy h3{font-size:clamp(24px,4vw,40px);line-height:1.3;margin:8px 0}.demo-copy p{color:#d5dbd1}.demo-shape{height:200px;border-radius:55% 45% 39% 61%;background:radial-gradient(circle at 35% 30%,#e7eccd 0 8%,#a4ba8b 9% 28%,#6d8a6b 29% 54%,#314d3d 55%);box-shadow:0 25px 50px #0003}.interactive-demo[data-theme=studio] .demo-shape{border-radius:18px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#e5e7e7 0 20%,#9ba4a5 21% 52%,#5d686b 53%)}.interactive-demo[data-theme=consult] .demo-shape{border-radius:50% 50% 12px 12px;background:linear-gradient(160deg,#e0c991 0 30%,#ab8e63 31% 55%,#5a4a36 56%)}
@media(max-width:650px){.wizard-progress{grid-template-columns:1fr}.wizard-progress li{padding:7px 10px}.interactive-demo{grid-template-columns:1fr;min-height:330px}.demo-shape{height:125px}.contact-form .extra-consent{align-items:flex-start}}
.whatsapp-float{position:fixed;z-index:35;right:18px;bottom:18px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 16px;border:1px solid #68ae88;border-radius:999px;background:#167a50;color:#fff;text-decoration:none;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;box-shadow:0 10px 30px #07140e55;transition:transform .2s,background .2s}.whatsapp-float:hover{transform:translateY(-3px);background:#126743}.whatsapp-float svg{width:24px;height:24px;flex:none;fill:none;stroke:currentColor;stroke-width:1.7;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round}.whatsapp-float span{display:grid;line-height:1.25}.whatsapp-float small{font:500 9px/1.5 var(--font);opacity:.83;direction:ltr;text-align:inherit}.whatsapp-float:focus-visible,.quick-contact:focus-visible{outline:3px solid #a8ba92;outline-offset:3px}@media(max-width:620px){.whatsapp-float{right:12px;bottom:64px;padding:9px 12px;font-size:11px}.whatsapp-float svg{width:22px;height:22px}.whatsapp-float small{font-size:8px}}

## system-status.html

```html
<!doctype html><html lang="ar" dir="rtl"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><meta name="description" content="تحقق من حالة موقع WW وخدمات استقبال الطلبات."><title>حالة الخدمات | WW</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="styles.css"><link rel="stylesheet" href="service-pages.css"></head><body><header class="site-header"><div class="container nav-wrap no-toggle"><a class="brand" href="index.html"><span class="brand-mark"><svg viewBox="0 0 64 52"><path d="M4 13 10 39 17 22 24 39 31 13M33 13 39 39 46 22 53 39 60 13"/></svg></span><span class="brand-name"><strong>WW</strong><small>DIGITAL SERVICES</small></span></a><nav class="main-nav"><a href="index.html">الرئيسية</a><a href="updates.html">التحديثات</a><a href="en/system-status.html" lang="en">English</a></nav></div></header><main><section class="inner-hero"><div class="container"><div class="eyebrow">حالة الخدمات</div><h1>حالة الموقع والطلبات.</h1><p>تعرض هذه الصفحة فحصًا آنيًا للموقع وقاعدة استقبال الطلبات. مزودات البريد والدفع ورفع الملفات غير مربوطة في الوقت الحالي.</p></div></section><section class="page-content"><div class="container"><div id="system-status" class="detail-grid" aria-live="polite"><article class="detail-card">جارٍ فحص الحالة…</article></div><p id="status-checked"></p><button class="pill-link" id="status-refresh" type="button">تحديث الحالة</button></div></section></main><script>const labels={website:'الموقع',requests:'استقبال الطلبات',emailNotifications:'إشعارات البريد',payments:'الدفع الإلكتروني',secureFilePortal:'بوابة الملفات'};async function refreshStatus(){const box=document.querySelector('#system-status');box.replaceChildren();try{const r=await fetch('/api/status',{cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)throw Error();const d=await r.json();for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(d.services)){const card=document.createElement('article');card.className='detail-card';const h=document.createElement('h2');h.textContent=labels[k]||k;const p=document.createElement('p');p.textContent=v==='operational'?'يعمل':v==='unavailable'?'يواجه مشكلة':'غير مفعّل';const badge=document.createElement('span');badge.className='status-badge';badge.textContent=p.textContent;card.append(h,badge);box.append(card);}document.querySelector('#status-checked').textContent='آخر فحص: '+new Date(d.checkedAt).toLocaleString('ar-SA');}catch{box.textContent='تعذر جلب حالة الخدمة حاليًا.';}}document.querySelector('#status-refresh').addEventListener('click',refreshStatus);refreshStatus();</script></body></html>
```

التكلفة النهائية والمتطلبات. لا تضمن إرشادات SEO ترتيبًا محددًا أو عدد زيارات. لا تهدف هذه السياسة إلى استبعاد مسؤولية أو حق لا يجوز استبعاده نظامًا.</p><p>للتفاصيل الكاملة، راجع أقسام السياسات في <a class="inner-hero"><div class="container"><div class="eyebrow">متابعة الطلب</div><h1>ملخص طلبك ومراحله الحالية.</h1><p>أدخل رقم الطلب الذي ظهر بعد الإرسال. ستظهر الخدمة والحالة والتاريخ فقط، دون style="display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:10px"><input id="order-id" name="id" required placeholder="WW-XXXXXXXXXX" dir="ltr" style="flex:1"><button class="pill-link" type="submit">عرض الملخص</button></div><div id="track-result" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div></form></div></section></main><script>const params=new URLSearchParams(location.search);const idInput=document.querySelector('#order-id');if(params.get('id'))idInput.value=params.get('id');document.querySelector('#track-form').addEventListener('submit',async e=>{e.preventDefault();const box=document.querySelector('#track-result');box.textContent='جارٍ البحث…';box.className='tool-result';try{const r=await fetch('/api/order-status/'+encodeURIComponent(idInput.value.trim()));const data=await r.json();if(!r.ok)throw new Error('لم نعثر على طلب بهذا الرقم.');const order=data.order;const services={'إنشاء نطاق':'إنشاء نطاق','إنشاء موقع':'إنشاء موقع','بريد أعمال':'بريد أعمال','جميع الخدمات':'جميع الخدمات','تذكير بتجديد نطاق':'طلب تذكير بتجديد نطاق','طلب موعد استشارة':'طلب موعد استشارة','تقييم خدمة':'تقييم خدمة','طلب اتصال':'طلب اتصال هاتفي','طلب عرض سعر':'طلب عرض سعر','صيانة موقع':'صيانة موقع'};const labels={new:'تم استلام الطلب',reviewing:'قيد المراجعة',awaiting_customer:'بانتظار معلومات منك',in_progress:'قيد التنفيذ',completed:'مكتمل',cancelled:'ملغي'};const stages=[['new','تم الاستلام'],['reviewing','المراجعة'],['in_progress','التنفيذ'],['completed','التسليم']];const active=order.status==='completed'?3:order.status==='in_progress'?2:order.status==='reviewing'||order.status==='awaiting_customer'?1:0;const card=document.createElement('div');card.className='status-result';const number=document.createElement('div');number.textContent='رقم الطلب';const id=document.createElement('div');id.className='request-number';id.textContent=order.requestNumber||order.id;const summary=document.createElement('p');summary.textContent=`الخدمة: ${services[order.service]||order.service} · الحالة: ${labels[order.status]||'قيد المعالجة'}`;const date=document.createElement('p');const when=/^[0-9]+$/.test(String(order.createdAt))?Number(order.createdAt)*1000:order.createdAt;date.textContent='تاريخ الطلب: '+new Date(when).toLocaleString('ar-SA');const list=document.createElement('ol');list.className='track-timeline';stages.forEach(([key,label],i)=>{const li=document.createElement('li');li.textContent=label;if(i<active)li.classList.add('done');if(i===active)li.classList.add('current');if(order.status==='completed')li.classList.add('done');if(key===order.status)li.setAttribute('aria-current','step');list.append(li);});card.append(number,id,summary,date,list);if(order.status==='awaiting_customer'){const wait=document.createElement('p');wait.textContent='نحتاج إلى معلومات إضافية لاستكمال الطلب. أرسل رقم الطلب عبر نموذج التواصل.';card.append(wait);}if(order.status==='cancelled'){const cancelled=document.createElement('p');cancelled.textContent='تم إغلاق الطلب. تواصل معنا إذا رغبت في الاستفسار.';card.append(cancelled);}if(order.status==='completed'){const feedback=document.createElement('a');feedback.className='pill-link';feedback.href='index.html?flow=review&order='+encodeURIComponent(order.requestNumber||order.id)+'#contact';feedback.textContent='شارك تقييمك عن الخدمة';card.append(feedback);}box.replaceChildren(card);}catch(err){box.className='tool-result class="inner-hero"><div class="container"><div class="eyebrow">قائمة تجهيز</div><h1>اجمع أساسيات موقعك في مكان واحد.</h1><p>علّم ما جهزته؛ تحفظ القائمة على هذا الجهاز في متصفحك، ويمكنك طباعتها أو العودة إليها لاحقًا.</p></div></section><section class="page-content"><div class="container"><div class="tool-card"><div id="checklist-items"><label><input type="checkbox" data-item> اسم النشاط وطريقة كتابته</label><label><input type="checkbox" data-item> وصف مختصر للنشاط والجمهور المستهدف</label><label><input type="checkbox" data-item> الخدمات أو المنتجات والأسعار المعتمدة</label><label><input type="checkbox" data-item> الشعار والألوان أو التوجه البصري</label><label><input type="checkbox" data-item> صور أملك حق استخدامها أو صور مرخصة</label><label><input type="checkbox" data-item> بيانات التواصل والعنوان إن رغبت بنشره</label><label><input type="checkbox" data-item> روابط حسابات التواصل الاجتماعي</label><label><input type="checkbox" data-item> النطاق المرغوب وبيانات الملكية</label><label><input type="checkbox" data-item> الصفحات المطلوبة وهدف كل صفحة</label><label><input type="checkbox" data-item> تحديد مسؤول مراجعة واعتماد المحتوى</label></div><p id="checklist-progress" role="status"></p><button class="pill-link" type="button" onclick="window.print()">طباعة القائمة</button></div><p class="form-hint">لا ترسل كلمات مرور أو بيانات دفع عبر نموذج التواصل. تُحدد المواد الداخلة في نطاق العمل في العرض المكتوب.</p></div></section></main><script>const key='ww-site-prep-v1';const items=[...document.querySelectorAll('[data-item]')];const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');items.forEach((item,i)=>{item.checked=Boolean(saved[i]);item.addEventListener('change',()=>{localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(items.map(x=>x.checked)));render();});});function render(){const done=items.filter(x=>x.checked).length;document.querySelector('#checklist-progress').textContent=`أنجزت ${done} من ${items.length} عناصر.`;}render();</script></body></html>
